the vector space of Schwartz distributions
V\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m},\mathscr{A})
such that there exists a kernel
V_{\mathrm{K}}\,:\,\mathbb{R}^{2}\times\mathrm{Borel}(\mathbb{R}^{2(m-1)})\to\mathscr{A}
satisfying the following conditions:

(1)

for every
A\in\mathrm{Borel}(\mathbb{R}^{2(m-1)})
the map
x\mapsto V_{\mathrm{K}}(x,A)
is Borel measurable,

(2)

for every
x\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
the map
A\mapsto V_{\mathrm{K}}(x,A)
is a Banach space valued measure,

(3)

the following norm

\|V\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}:=\sup_{x_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2(m-1)}}\|V_{\mathrm{K}}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2}\ldots\mathrm{d}x_{m})\|_{\mathscr{B}}

(10.6)

is finite, where
\|V_{\mathrm{K}}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2}\ldots\mathrm{d}x_{m})\|_{\mathscr{B}}
denotes the variation of
V_{\mathrm{K}}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2}\ldots\mathrm{d}x_{m})
,

(4)

for all
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})
it holds

\langle V,\varphi\rangle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2m}}V_{\mathrm{K}}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2}\ldots\mathrm{d}x_{m})\,\varphi(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m})\,\mathrm{d}x_{1}.

(10.7)

We write
\mathscr{M}^{m}:=\mathscr{M}^{m}(\mathbb{C})
.

Remark 10.7
.

Note that it holds
\mathscr{M}^{m}(\mathbb{C})\subset\mathscr{M}^{m}(\mathscr{A})\subset\mathscr{M}^{m}(\mathscr{B})
.

Remark 10.8
.

Let
K\,:\,\mathbb{R}^{2m}\to\mathscr{B}
be measurable such that

\|K\|_{\mathscr{K}^{m}}:=\sup_{x_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2(m-1)}}\|K(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m})\|_{\mathscr{B}}\,\mathrm{d}x_{2}\ldots\mathrm{d}x_{m}<\infty.

(10.8)

Then
V\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m},\mathscr{B})
defined by Eq. (
10.7
) with

V_{\mathrm{K}}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2}\ldots\mathrm{d}x_{m})=K(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m})\,\mathrm{d}x_{2}\ldots\mathrm{d}x_{m}

(10.9)

belongs to
\mathscr{M}^{m}(\mathscr{B})
and
\|V\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}=\|K\|_{\mathscr{K}^{m}}
.

Remark 10.9
.

Let
\delta^{(m)}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})
be the Dirac measure on the diagonal, i.e.

\langle\delta^{(m)},\varphi_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{m}\rangle:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\varphi_{1}(x)\ldots\varphi_{m}(x)\,\mathrm{d}x

(10.10)

for all
\varphi_{1},\ldots,\varphi_{m}\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
. Then
\delta^{(m)}\in\mathscr{M}^{m}
and
\|\delta^{(m)}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}=1
.

Remark 10.10
.

It follows from the definition of the variation of the vector measure and the properties (0) and (4) of the conditional expected value introduced in Def.
6.7
that

\mathbf{E}_{t}V\in\mathscr{M}^{m}(\mathscr{F}_{t}),\qquad\|\mathbf{E}_{t}V\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\leq\|V\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}

(10.11)

for all
V\in\mathscr{M}^{m}(\mathscr{F})
and
t\in[0,1]
.

Remark 10.11
.

Recall that
w_{1}^{m}
and
\tilde{w}_{1}^{m}
are weights introduced in Def.
9.1
.

Definition 10.12
.

Let
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
\mathscr{A}
be a unital Banach subalgebra of
\mathscr{B}
. The vector space
\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{m}(\mathscr{A})\subset\mathscr{M}^{m}(\mathscr{A})^{\mathbb{A}^{m}\times\mathbb{G}^{m}}
consists of Schwartz distributions

V\equiv(V^{a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m},\mathscr{A})^{\mathbb{A}^{m}\times\mathbb{G}^{m}}

(10.12)

such that the following norm

\|V\|_{\mathscr{N}^{m}}:=\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|w_{1}^{m}V^{a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}

(10.13)

is finite. We define the norm
\|V\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}
in an analogous way with
w_{1}^{m}
replaced by
\tilde{w}_{1}^{m}
. We denote by
\mathscr{N}^{m}(\mathscr{A})
the subspace of
\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{m}(\mathscr{A})
consisting of antisymmetric Schwartz distributions, cf. Def.
10.4
. The vector space
\mathscr{N}(\mathscr{A}):=\bigtimes_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\mathscr{N}^{m}(\mathscr{A})
consists of tuples

V\equiv(V^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\equiv(V^{m,a,\sigma})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+},a\in\mathbb{A}^{m},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}.

(10.14)

We endow
\mathscr{N}(\mathscr{A})
with the product topology. The subspace
\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}(\mathscr{A})\subset\mathscr{N}(\mathscr{A})
consists of tuples
V
such that
V^{m}=0
for all but finitely many
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. We set
\Pi^{m}V:=V^{m}

and
\Pi^{m,a,\sigma}V:=V^{m,a,\sigma}
. For
V\in\mathscr{N}(\mathscr{A})
and
k\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
we define
\Pi_{k}V\equiv((\Pi_{k}V)^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\in\mathscr{N}(\mathscr{A})
by the equalities
(\Pi_{k}V)^{m}:=0
for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus\{k\}
and
(\Pi_{k}V)^{m}:=V^{m}
for
m=k
. We also define
\Pi_{>k}V\equiv((\Pi_{>k}V)^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\in\mathscr{N}(\mathscr{A})
by the equalities
(\Pi_{>k}V)^{m}:=0
for all
m\in\{1,\ldots,k\}
and
(\Pi_{>k}V)^{m}:=V^{m}
for
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus\{1,\ldots,k\}
. For
V\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}(\mathscr{A})
and
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2},\mathscr{A})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}
we write

V[\varphi]:=\sum_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\langle V^{m},\varphi^{\otimes m}\rangle\in\mathscr{A}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}.

(10.15)

For
k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
we define
\mathrm{D}^{k}_{\varphi}V[\varphi]
in analogy to Def.
3.14
. We set
\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{m}:=\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{m}(\mathbb{C})
,
\mathscr{N}^{m}:=\mathscr{N}^{m}(\mathbb{C})
,
\mathscr{N}:=\mathscr{N}(\mathbb{C})
and
\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}:=\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}(\mathbb{C})
.

Remark 10.13
.

Let
V\in\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{m}(\mathscr{A})
. Since the norm
\|V\|_{\mathscr{N}^{m}}<\infty
involves a weight of stretched exponential growth it holds
\mathcal{X}V^{a,\sigma}\in\mathscr{M}
for all
a\in\mathbb{A}^{m},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}
and all translationally-invariant polynomials
\mathcal{X}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})
.

Remark 10.14
.

Using the fact that
V\in\mathscr{N}^{m}(\mathscr{A})
is antisymmetric one shows that it is uniquely determined by the map

\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}\ni\varphi\mapsto\langle V,\varphi^{\otimes m}\rangle\in\mathscr{A}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}.

(10.16)

Actually, given
\psi_{1},\ldots,\psi_{m}\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}
we choose
\varphi=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\psi_{j}g_{j}
, where
g_{1},\ldots,g_{m}\in\mathscr{G}
are such that
g=g_{1}\ldots g_{m}\neq 0
. Then
m!\,\langle V,\psi_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\psi_{m}\rangle\otimes g=\langle V,\varphi^{\otimes m}\rangle
.

Definition 10.15
.

Let
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
,
\alpha\in(0,\infty)
,
\beta,\gamma\in[0,\infty)
and

\rho_{\gamma,\kappa}(m):=\gamma+2\kappa m.

For continuous maps

(0,1]\ni s\mapsto V^{m}_{s}\equiv(V^{m,a,\sigma}_{s})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\in\mathscr{N}^{m}(\mathscr{B})

(10.17)

we define

\|V^{m}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}:=\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\sup_{s\in(0,1]}\lambda_{s}^{-\rho_{\gamma,\kappa}(m)}\,s^{2-m/2-|a|}\,\|w^{m}_{s}V_{s}^{m,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}.

(10.18)

The vector space
\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}
consists of continuous maps

(0,1]\ni s\mapsto V^{m}_{s}\equiv(V^{m,a,\sigma}_{s})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\in\mathscr{N}^{m}\equiv\mathscr{N}^{m}(\mathbb{C})

(10.19)

such that
\|V^{m}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}<\infty
. The vector space
\mathscr{V}^{\gamma}
consists of continuous maps

(0,1]\ni s\mapsto V_{s}\in\mathscr{N}\equiv\mathscr{N}(\mathbb{C})

(10.20)

such that
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}^{m}\equiv\Pi^{m}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}
for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. The vector space
\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma}
consists of continuous maps

(0,1]\ni s\mapsto V_{s}\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}\equiv\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}(\mathbb{C})

(10.21)

such that
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}^{m}\equiv\Pi^{m}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}
for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. We denote by
\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
the closure of
\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma}\subset\mathscr{V}^{\gamma}
with respect to the norm

\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}:=\sup_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\alpha^{m}\,m^{\beta}\,\|V^{m}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}.

(10.22)

For
V^{m}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}
we define
\|V^{m}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{m;\gamma}}
in an analogous way to
\|V^{m}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}
with
w^{m}_{s}
replaced by
\tilde{w}^{m}_{s}
in Eq. (
10.18
). For
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
we define
\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
in an analogous way to
\|V^{m}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
with
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}
replaced by
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{m;\gamma}}
in Eq. (
10.22
).

Remark 10.16
.

Since
\tilde{w}^{m}_{t}\leq w^{m}_{t}
the norms
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{m;\gamma}}
and
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
are weaker than the norms
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}
and
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
, respectively. The norms
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{m;\gamma}}
and
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
will be used to study the infinite volume limit. Observe that we did not define the spaces
\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{m;\gamma}
and
\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
.

Remark 10.17
.

Note that the weight
\tilde{w}^{m}_{t}
used in the definition of the norms
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{m;\gamma}}
and
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
is not invariant under permutations of its arguments. For a permutation
\pi\in\mathcal{P}_{m}
define
\mathbf{S}_{\pi}\tilde{w}^{m}_{t}\in C(\mathbb{R}^{2m})
by the equality
(\mathbf{S}_{\pi}\tilde{w}^{m}_{t})(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m}):=\tilde{w}^{m}_{t}(x_{\pi(1)},\ldots,x_{\pi(m)})
. Using the fact that

V^{m}\equiv(V^{m,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\in\mathscr{N}^{m}(\mathscr{B})\subset\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m},\mathscr{A})^{\mathbb{A}^{m}\times\mathbb{G}^{m}}

(10.23)

is antisymmetric one shows that

\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|(\mathbf{S}_{\pi}\tilde{w}^{m}_{s})V^{m,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}=\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|\tilde{w}^{m}_{s}V^{m,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}.

(10.24)

Remark 10.18
.

For all
\alpha,\tilde{\alpha}\in[1,\infty)
,
\beta,\tilde{\beta},\gamma,\tilde{\gamma}\in[0,\infty)
and
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
such that
\tilde{\alpha}\leq\alpha
,
\tilde{\beta}<\beta
,
\tilde{\gamma}\leq\gamma
it holds

\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\beta};\tilde{\gamma}}}\leq\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}},\qquad\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\beta};\tilde{\gamma}}}\leq\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}.

(10.25)

Remark 10.19
.

For all
\alpha,\beta,\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\beta}\in[1,\infty)
,
k\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\gamma\in[0,\infty)
and
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
it holds

\|\Pi^{k}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{k;\gamma}}\leq C\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}},\qquad\|\Pi_{k}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\beta};\gamma}}\leq C\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}.

(10.26)

Lemma 10.20
.

For all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
\alpha\in(0,\infty)
,
\beta,\gamma\in[0,\infty)
the spaces
(\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma},\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}})
and
(\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma},\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}})
are Banach spaces.

Proof.

We first observe that for every
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
the space
(\mathscr{M}^{m}(\mathbb{C}),\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}})
is a Banach space and given a Banach space
\mathscr{T}
the spaces
C_{\mathrm{b}}((0,1],\mathscr{T})
and
l^{\infty}_{0}(\mathscr{T})
are also Banach spaces. Using the fact that
\lambda_{s}^{-\rho_{\gamma,\kappa}(m)}\,s^{2-m/2-|a|}\,w^{m}_{s}\neq 0
for all
s\in(0,1]
,
a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}
we conclude that the space
(\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma},\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}})
is a Banach space. Similarly, since
\alpha^{m}\,m^{\beta}\neq 0
for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
the space
(\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma},\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}})
is a Banach space.
∎

Remark 10.21
.

Recall that
\mathscr{F}
is the Banach subalgebra of
\mathscr{B}=\mathscr{B}(\mathscr{H})
generated by the white noise, cf. Def.
6.2
.

Definition 10.22
.

Let
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t,s\in(0,1]
. The maps

\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s},\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\,:\,\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}(\mathscr{F})\to\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}(\mathscr{F})

are defined by the equalities

\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V[\varphi]:=V[\varphi+\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}]-V[\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}],\qquad\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-},

(10.27)

\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V:=\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V-V,

(10.28)

where
\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
was introduced in Def.
6.11
and
\mathbf{J}
is the jet prolongation introduced in Def.
8.1
. We also set
\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}:=\Pi^{m}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
,
\mathbf{A}^{m,a}_{t,s}:=\Pi^{m,a}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
and
\mathbf{A}^{m,a,\sigma}_{t,s}:=\Pi^{m,a,\sigma}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
and analogously for the map
\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
. We omit
\tau
and
\varepsilon
if
\tau=0
and
\varepsilon=0
.

Remark 10.23
.

The equation defining the map
\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
should be interpreted as

\langle\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V,\varphi^{\otimes m}\rangle=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\frac{(m+k)!}{m!k!}\,\langle V^{m+k},\varphi^{\otimes m}\otimes\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}^{\otimes k}\rangle

(10.29)

for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. The map
\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
is well defined thanks to Remark
10.14
and the fact that
\|\mathbf{J}\varPsi^{a,\sigma}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}\|_{\mathscr{C}}<\infty
for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
,
t,s\in(0,1]
and
a\in\mathbb{A}
,
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}
, which follows from Lemma
6.20
.

Remark 10.24
.

Let
t,s\in(0,1]
be such that
t\geq s
and let
V\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}(\mathscr{F}_{s})
. Then we have

\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}(\mathscr{F}_{t}),\qquad\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V=\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V,\qquad\mathbf{E}_{s}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V=\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{E}V

(10.30)

by Def.
6.11
of
\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
and the properties (1) and (3) of the conditional expected value introduced in Def.
6.7
.

Remark 10.25
.

Let
t,u,s\in(0,1]
be such that
t\geq u\geq s
and let
V\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}(\mathbb{C})
. Then we have

(A)

\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V=\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,u}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u,s}V
,

(B)

\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,u}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u,s}V=\mathbf{E}_{u}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,u}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u,s}V=\mathbf{E}_{u}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V
,

(C)

\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,u}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u,s}V=\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V

by Remark
10.24
and the tower property (2) of the conditional expected value introduced in Def.
6.7
.

Lemma 10.26
.

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
the following is true. Let
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
,
\alpha,\beta\in[1,\infty)
,
\gamma\in[0,\infty)
,
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma}
. For all
t\in(0,1]
the map

(0,1]\ni s\mapsto\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V_{s}\in\mathscr{N}^{m}(\mathscr{F}_{t,s})

(10.31)

is continuous and it holds

(A)

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V_{s}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha/2,\beta;\gamma}}\leq\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
,

(B)

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V_{s}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha/2,\beta-1;\gamma}}\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
,

(C)

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V_{s}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha/2,\beta;\gamma}}\leq\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
,

(D)

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V_{s}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha/2,\beta-1;\gamma}}\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
,

(E)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{A}_{t,s}-\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s})V_{s}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha/2,\beta-1;\gamma}}\leq\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
.

In particular the map (
10.31
) is well-defined for all
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
.

Remark 10.27
.

Note that the parameters
\alpha
of the norms
\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
,
\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
that appear on both sides of the bounds stated in the above lemma are different. As we discussed in Sec.
2
the bounds of this type appear to be false if the parameters
\alpha
of the norms are the same on both sides of the bounds. This is the main reason why we work with the space
(\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon},\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}})
introduced below. One of the consequences of the above lemma is the relation between the norms
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
and
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{2\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
stated in Remark
10.35
.

Proof.

First recall that
\lambda_{s}\leq\lambda
by Def.
2.2
and

\|\mathbf{J}\varPsi^{a,\sigma}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\|_{\mathscr{C}}\leq\lambda^{-\kappa}\,s^{-1/2},\qquad\|\tilde{w}(\mathbf{J}\varPsi^{a,\sigma}_{t,s}-\mathbf{J}\varPsi^{a,\sigma}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s})\|_{\mathscr{C}}\leq\lambda^{-\kappa}\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\lambda_{s}^{-\kappa}\,s^{-1/2}

(10.32)

by Lemma
6.20
provided
\lambda\in(0,1]
is small enough. Noting that

\rho_{\gamma,\kappa}(m)=\rho_{\gamma,\kappa}(m+k)-2\kappa k

and using Lemma
9.5
(d) we obtain

\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\lambda_{s}^{-\rho_{\gamma,\kappa}(m)}\,s^{2-m/2-|a|}\,\|w^{m}_{s}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}^{m,a,\sigma}V_{s}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\\
\leq\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m+k}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m+k}}\frac{(m+k)!}{m!k!}\,\lambda_{s}^{-\rho_{\gamma,\kappa}(m+k)+\kappa k}\,s^{2-m/2-k/2-|a|}\,\|w^{m+k}_{s}\,V_{s}^{m+k,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m+k}}.

(10.33)

Using Definition
10.15
of the norms
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m+k;\gamma}}
and
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
we estimate the expression on the RHS of the above bound by

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\frac{(m+k)!}{m!k!}\,\lambda^{\kappa k}\,\|V^{m+k}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m+k;\gamma}}\leq\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\frac{(m+k)!}{m!k!}\,\lambda^{\kappa k}\,\alpha^{-m-k}(m+k)^{-\beta}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}\\
\leq\alpha^{-m}\,m^{-\beta}\,(1-\lambda^{\kappa})^{-m-1}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}\leq 2^{m}\,\alpha^{-m}m^{-\beta}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}.

(10.34)

The last bound above is true of
\lambda^{\kappa}\leq 1/5
. This proves the bound (A). Using analogous estimates as above with the sum over
k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
replaced by the sum over
k\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
we obtain

\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\lambda_{s}^{-\rho_{\gamma,\kappa}(m)}\,s^{2-m/2-|a|}\,\|w^{m}_{s}(\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}^{m,a,\sigma}V_{s}-V_{s}^{m,a,\sigma})\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\\
\leq\alpha^{-m}\,(m+1)^{-\beta}\,((1-\lambda^{\kappa})^{-m-1}-1)\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,2^{m}\,\alpha^{-m}m^{1-\beta}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}},

(10.35)

where again the last bound above is true of
\lambda^{\kappa}\leq 1/5
. This implies the bound (B). To prove the bounds (C) and (D) we use exactly the same strategy but with the weight
w^{m}_{s}
replaced by
\tilde{w}^{m}_{s}
. Let us turn to the proof of the bound (E). By Lemma
9.5
(e) we have

\lambda^{\kappa}\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{-\kappa}\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\lambda_{s}^{-\rho_{\gamma,\kappa}(m)}\,s^{2-m/2-|a|}\,\|\tilde{w}^{m}_{s}(\mathbf{A}_{t,s}^{m,a,\sigma}-\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}^{m,a,\sigma})V_{s}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\\
\leq\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m+k}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m+k}}\frac{(m+k)!}{m!(k-1)!}\,\lambda_{s}^{-\rho_{\gamma,\kappa}(m+k)+\kappa k}\,s^{2-m/2-k/2-|a|}\,\|w^{m+k}_{s}\,V_{s}^{m+k,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m+k}}.

(10.36)

Using again Definition
10.15
of the norms
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m+k;\gamma}}
and
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
we estimate the expression on the RHS of the above bound by

\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\frac{(m+k)!}{m!(k-1)!}\,\lambda^{\kappa k}\,\|V^{m+k}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m+k;\gamma}}\leq\sum_{k\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\frac{(m+k)!}{m!(k-1)!}\,\lambda^{\kappa k}\,\alpha^{-m-k}(m+k)^{-\beta}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}\\
\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,(m+1)\,\alpha^{-m}(m+1)^{-\beta}\,(1-\lambda^{\kappa})^{-m-2}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,2^{m}\,\alpha^{-m}m^{1-\beta}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}.

(10.37)

The last bound above is true of
\lambda^{\kappa}\leq 1/5
. This proves the bound (E).
∎

Definition 10.28
.

We say that a distribution
V\in\mathscr{N}^{m}\subset\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{m}
is compatible with the flow of charge iff
V=0
for
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus 2\mathbb{N}_{+}
and otherwise it holds
V=\mathbf{S}W
for some
W\in\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{m}
such that:

(a)

\langle W,\varphi_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{m}\rangle=\langle W,\mathbf{C}\varphi_{1}\otimes\mathbf{C}\varphi_{2}\otimes\varphi_{3}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{m}\rangle
,

(b)

\langle W,\varphi_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{m}\rangle=\langle W,\varphi_{2\pi(1)-1}\otimes\varphi_{2\pi(1)}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{2\pi(m/2)-1}\otimes\varphi_{2\pi(m/2)}\rangle
,

(c)

\langle W,\varphi_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{m}\rangle=-\langle W,\varphi_{2}\otimes\varphi_{1}\otimes\varphi_{3}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{m}\rangle

for all
\varphi_{1},\ldots,\varphi_{m}\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}
and
\pi\in\mathcal{P}_{m/2}
.

Remark 10.29
.

We say that
V\in\mathscr{N}
is invariant under a certain symmetry or compatible with the flow of charge iff
V^{m}\in\mathscr{N}^{m}\subset\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{A}^{m}\times\mathbb{G}^{m}}
is invariant under this symmetry or compatible with the flow of charge for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
.

Remark 10.30
.

The map
\mathbf{C}
was introduced in Def.
8.6
. Note that if
V\in\mathscr{N}^{m}
is compatible with the flow of charge, then it is invariant under the charge conjugation. If
m\in\{4,6,\ldots\}
, then generically the reverse implication is false. Observe also that the second and the third of the functionals (
8.11
) are not compatible with the flow of charge. Hence, there are only three relevant or marginal local functionals invariant under all the symmetries listed in Sec.
8
and compatible with the flow of charge: the quadratic functionals (
8.10
) and the first of the quartic functionals (
8.11
). The reduction of the number of relevant or marginal local functionals that have to be investigated is the main reason behind introducing the notion of the compatibility with the flow of charge. See
[
GK85a
, Sec. 5.B]
for a simple argument showing that perturbative corrections to an effective potential are compatible with the flow of charge.

Definition 10.31
.

Let
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. We denote by
\mathscr{N}^{m}_{+}
the subspace of
\mathscr{N}^{m}
consisting of Schwartz distributions invariant under the symmetries of the torus and the internal symmetries and compatible with the flow of charge. We denote by
\mathscr{N}^{m}_{0}
the subspace of
\mathscr{N}^{m}_{+}
consisting of Schwartz distributions invariant under the symmetries of the plane. The closures of
\mathscr{N}^{m}_{+}
and
\mathscr{N}^{m}_{0}
in
\mathscr{N}^{m}
are denoted by
(\mathscr{N}^{m}_{+})^{\mathrm{c}}
and
(\mathscr{N}^{m}_{0})^{\mathrm{c}}
, respectively. Let
\mathrm{sgn}(\tau):=0
if
\tau=0
and
\mathrm{sgn}(\tau):=+
if
\tau\in(0,1]
. For
\tau\in(0,1]
we set
\mathscr{N}^{m}_{\tau}:=\mathscr{N}^{m}_{\mathrm{sgn}(\tau)}
and

\displaystyle\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}_{\tau}

\displaystyle:=\{V\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}\,|\,\forall_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}V^{m}\in\mathscr{N}^{m}_{\tau}\},

(10.38)

\displaystyle\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma}_{\tau}

\displaystyle:=\{V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma}\,|\,\forall_{s\in(0,1]}V_{s}\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}_{\tau}\}.

Definition 10.32
.

Let
\alpha,\beta\in[1,\infty)
,
\gamma\in[0,\infty)
and
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
. The vector space
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
is the closure of
\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma}_{\tau}\subset\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
with respect to the following norm

\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}:=\sup_{t\in[0,1]}\|s\mapsto\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}V_{s}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}.

(10.39)

For
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
we define
\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
in an analogous way to
\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
with
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
replaced by
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
in Eq. (
10.39
).

Remark 10.33
.

By Remark
10.16
the norm
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
is weaker than the norm
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
. The former norm will be used to study the infinite volume limit.

Remark 10.34
.

Note that

\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}\leq\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}},\qquad\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}\leq\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}.

(10.40)

Thus, in particular
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\subset\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
.

Remark 10.35
.

Observe that by Lemma
10.26
(A), (C) it holds

\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{2\alpha,\beta;\gamma}},\qquad\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{2\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}.

(10.41)

Remark 10.36
.

Note that
\mathscr{N}^{m}_{\tau}
,
\tau\in[0,1]
, is not a closed subspace of
\mathscr{N}^{m}
. It is easy to see that the invariance under the symmetries of the torus/plane, the internal symmetries and the charge conjugation symmetry are preserved under limits. However, this is not the case for the compatibility with the flow of charge. Hence, in general, it need not be true that for
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
it holds
V^{m}_{s}\in\mathscr{N}^{m}_{\tau}
for
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
s\in(0,1]
. Nonetheless,
V^{m}_{s}\in(\mathscr{N}^{m}_{\tau})^{\mathrm{c}}
for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
s\in(0,1]
.

Lemma 10.37
.

For all
\alpha,\beta\in[1,\infty)
,
\gamma\in[0,\infty)
and
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
the space
(\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon},\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}})
is a Banach space.

Proof.

Let
({}^{p}V)_{p\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}
be a Cauchy sequence in
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
. Then,
({}^{p}V)_{p\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}
is a Cauchy sequence in
\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
. Consequently, there exists
V\in\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
such that

\lim_{p\to\infty}\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-({}^{p}V)_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}=0.

(10.42)

By Lemma
10.26
(A) it holds

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}(({}^{p}V)_{s}-V_{s})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}\leq 2^{m}\alpha^{m}m^{-\beta}\,\|({}^{p}V)_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}.

(10.43)

Let
\delta\in(0,\infty)
. There exists
P\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
such that for all
p,q\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
,
p,q\geq P
it holds

\|({}^{p}V)_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-({}^{q}V)_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\delta

(10.44)

and for every
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
there exists
p(m)\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
,
p(m)\geq P
, such that

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}(({}^{p(m)}V)_{s}-V_{s})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}\leq\alpha^{-m}m^{-\beta}\,\delta.

(10.45)

As a result, for all
p\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
,
p\geq P
it holds

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}(V_{s}-({}^{p}V)_{s})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}\\
\leq\|s\mapsto\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}(V_{s}-({}^{p(m)}V)_{s})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}+\|s\mapsto\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}(({}^{p(m)}V)_{s}-({}^{p}V)_{s})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}\\
\leq\alpha^{-m}m^{-\beta}\,\delta+\alpha^{-m}m^{-\beta}\,\|({}^{p}V)_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-({}^{q}V)_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq 2\,\alpha^{-m}m^{-\beta}\,\delta.

(10.46)

Hence,
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and
\lim_{p\to\infty}\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-({}^{p}V)_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}=0
.
∎

11
Local part and remainder

In this section we introduce operators
\mathbf{L}
,
\mathbf{L}_{\partial}
and
\mathbf{R}
and establish their most important properties. The operators
\mathbf{L}
,
\mathbf{L}_{\partial}
and
\mathbf{R}
are used to decompose kernels of functionals into the local part and the remainder. As we discussed in Sec.
2
the above-mentioned decomposition is crucial for the solution of the renormalization problem.

Definition 11.1
.

The distributions

U^{2}\equiv(U^{2,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}},\,U_{\partial}^{2}\equiv(U_{\partial}^{2,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}_{0}\subset\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{4})^{\mathbb{A}^{2}\times\mathbb{G}^{2}},

(11.1)

such that for all
a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}
and
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
it holds
U^{2,a,\sigma}=0
unless
|a|=0
and
U_{\partial}^{2,a,\sigma}=0
unless
|a|=1
are defined by the equalities

\begin{gathered}\langle U^{2},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\bar{\psi}(x)\cdot\underaccent{\bar}{\psi}(x)\,\mathrm{d}x,\\
\langle U_{\partial}^{2},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\bar{\psi}(x)\cdot((\Gamma_{1}\partial_{1}+\Gamma_{2}\partial_{2})\underaccent{\bar}{\psi})(x)\,\mathrm{d}x\end{gathered}

(11.2)

for all
\psi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
, where
\mathbf{J}
is the jet prolongation introduced in Def.
8.1
. The distribution

U^{4}\equiv(U^{4,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{4},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}_{0}\subset\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{8})^{\mathbb{A}^{4}\times\mathbb{G}^{4}}

(11.3)

such that for all
a\in\mathbb{A}^{4}
and
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}
it holds
U^{4,a,\sigma}=0
unless
|a|=0
is defined by the equalities

\langle U^{4},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\bar{\psi}(x)\cdot\underaccent{\bar}{\psi}(x))^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x

(11.4)

for all
\psi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
. For
g,r,z\in\mathbb{C}
we define
U(g,r,z)\in\mathscr{N}
by the equation

U(g,r,z)[\psi]:=g\,\langle U^{4},\psi^{\otimes 4}\rangle+r\,\langle U^{2},\psi^{\otimes 2}\rangle+z\,\langle U_{\partial}^{2},\psi^{\otimes 2}\rangle

(11.5)

for all
\psi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
.

Remark 11.2
.

Note that
U(g,r,z)=g\,U(1,0,0)+r\,U(0,1,0)+z\,U(0,0,1)
.

Remark 11.3
.

Recall that by Def.
10.31
distributions belonging to the space
\mathscr{N}^{m}_{0}\subset\mathscr{N}^{m}\subset\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{A}^{m}\times\mathbb{G}^{m}}
are invariant under the symmetries of the plane and the internal symmetries and compatible with the flow of charge. Moreover, distributions belonging to
\mathscr{N}^{m}
are antisymmetric. Using the above-mentioned properties one proves that
U^{2},U^{2}_{\partial},U^{4}
are uniquely defined by the equalities stated in Def.
11.1
. In particular, by the charge conjugation invariance and Remark
4.3
it holds

\langle U_{\partial}^{2},\psi^{\otimes 2}\rangle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big(\bar{\psi}^{0}(x)\cdot\Gamma_{i}\underaccent{\bar}{\psi}^{a_{i}}(x)-\bar{\psi}^{a_{i}}(x)\cdot\Gamma_{i}\underaccent{\bar}{\psi}^{0}(x)\big)\,\mathrm{d}x

(11.6)

for all
\psi=(\bar{\psi}^{a},\underaccent{\bar}{\psi}^{a})_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
and
a_{1}=(1,0)\in\mathbb{A}
,
a_{2}=(0,1)\in\mathbb{A}
.

Remark 11.4
.

It holds

•

if
\sigma=((-,1,1),(+,1,1))\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
, then
U^{2,\sigma,0}=\delta^{(2)}/2
,

•

if
a=((0,0),(1,0))\in\mathbb{A}^{2}
,
\sigma=((-,1,1),(+,1,2))\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
, then
U^{2,\sigma,a}=\delta^{(2)}/4
,

•

if
\sigma=((-,1,1),(+,1,1),(-,1,1),(+,1,1))\in\mathbb{G}^{4}
, then
U^{4,\sigma,0}=\delta^{(4)}/6
,

where
\delta^{(m)}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})
is the Dirac measure on the diagonal introduced in Remark
10.9
.

Remark 11.5
.

In the definitions stated below we use the fact that for every distribution
V\in\mathscr{M}^{m}\subset\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})
there exists a kernel
V_{\mathrm{K}}
in the sense of Definition
10.6
such that Eq. (
10.7
) holds. Note that the maps introduced in the definitions below do not depend on the choice of the kernel
V_{\mathrm{K}}
associated to a distribution
V
.

Definition 11.6
.

Let
V^{2}=(V^{2,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}
and
V^{4}=(V^{4,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{4},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}
be translationally invariant. We define
\mathbf{L}V^{2}\in\mathbb{C}
by the equality

\mathbf{L}V^{2}:=2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}V^{2,0,\sigma}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2})

(11.7)

with
\sigma=((-,1,1),(+,1,1))\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
. We define
\mathbf{L}_{\partial}V^{2}:=\hat{\mathbf{L}}_{\partial}V^{2}+\check{\mathbf{L}}_{\partial}V^{2}\in\mathbb{C}
by the equalities

\hat{\mathbf{L}}_{\partial}V^{2}:=4\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(x_{2}-x_{1})^{a_{1}+a_{2}}\,V^{2,0,\sigma}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2}),\qquad\check{\mathbf{L}}_{\partial}V^{2}:=4\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}V^{2,a,\sigma}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2}),

(11.8)

with
a=(a_{1},a_{2})=((0,0),(1,0))\in\mathbb{A}^{2}
and
\sigma=((-,1,1),(+,1,2))\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
. We define
\mathbf{L}V^{4}\in\mathbb{C}
by the equality

\mathbf{L}V^{4}:=6\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}}V^{4,0,\sigma}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2},\mathrm{d}x_{3},\mathrm{d}x_{4})

(11.9)

with
\sigma=((-,1,1),(+,1,1),(-,1,1),(+,1,1))\in\mathbb{G}^{4}
.

Remark 11.7
.

By translational invariance the above definitions of
\mathbf{L}V^{2},\mathbf{L}_{\partial}V^{2},\mathbf{L}V^{4}
do not depend on the choice of
x_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
.

Remark 11.8
.

Note that the following equalities
\mathbf{L}U^{2}=1
,
\mathbf{L}_{\partial}U_{\partial}^{2}=1
,
\mathbf{L}U^{4}=1
hold true.

Lemma 11.9
.

Let
\nu\in[0,1/2]
. There exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
t\in(0,1]
and all translationally invariant
V^{2}=(V^{2,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}
,
V^{4}=(V^{4,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{4},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}
it holds:

(A)

|\mathbf{L}V^{2}|\leq\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\|w^{2}_{t;\nu}V^{2,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}}
,

(B)

|\mathbf{L}_{\partial}V^{2}|\leq C\,t\,\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\|w^{2}_{t;\nu}V^{2,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}}
,

(C)

|\mathbf{L}V^{4}|\leq\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{4}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}}\|w^{4}_{t;\nu}V^{4,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{4}}
.

Proof.

By Remark
11.7
we can set
x_{1}=0
in the equations defining
\mathbf{L}V^{2},\mathbf{L}_{\partial}V^{2},\mathbf{L}V^{4}
, which are given in Def.
11.6
. The bounds follow now immediately from Def.
10.6
of the norm
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}
and the fact that there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that
|x_{2}-x_{1}|/w^{2}_{t;\nu}(x_{1},x_{2})\leq C\,t
for
x_{1}=0
and all
x_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
and
t\in(0,1]
.
∎

Lemma 11.10
.

Let
V^{2}=(V^{2,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\in(\mathscr{N}^{2}_{+})^{\mathrm{c}}
,
V^{4}=(V^{4,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{4},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}}\in(\mathscr{N}^{4}_{+})^{\mathrm{c}}
.
Then we have

\mathbf{L}V^{2},\hat{\mathbf{L}}_{\partial}V^{2},\check{\mathbf{L}}_{\partial}V^{2},\mathbf{L}_{\partial}V^{4}\in\mathbb{R}

(11.10)

and the following equalities

\mathbf{L}V^{2}\,\langle U^{2},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle=\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}V^{2,0,\sigma}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2})\,\psi^{\sigma_{1}}(x_{1})\,\psi^{\sigma_{2}}(x_{1})\,\mathrm{d}x_{1},

(11.11)

\hat{\mathbf{L}}_{\partial}V^{2}\,\langle U^{2}_{\partial},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\\
=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}\\
|a|=1\end{subarray}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}(x_{2}-x_{1})^{a_{1}+a_{2}}\,V^{2,0,\sigma}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2})\,(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{1},\sigma_{1}}(x_{1})\,(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{2},\sigma_{2}}(x_{1})\,\mathrm{d}x_{1},

(11.12)

\check{\mathbf{L}}_{\partial}V^{2}\,\langle U^{2}_{\partial},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}\\
|a|=1\end{subarray}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}V^{2,a,\sigma}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2})\,(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{1},\sigma_{1}}(x_{1})\,(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{2},\sigma_{2}}(x_{1})\,\mathrm{d}x_{1},

(11.13)

\mathbf{L}V^{4}\,\langle U^{4},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle=\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{8}}V^{4,0,\sigma}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2},\mathrm{d}x_{3},\mathrm{d}x_{4})\,\psi^{\sigma_{1}}(x_{1})\,\psi^{\sigma_{2}}(x_{1})\,\psi^{\sigma_{3}}(x_{1})\,\psi^{\sigma_{4}}(x_{1})\,\mathrm{d}x_{1}

(11.14)

hold true for all
\psi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
.

Remark 11.11
.

Recall that
\mathbf{J}\psi^{a,\sigma}\equiv(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a,\sigma}
is the jet prolongation. In particular,
\psi^{\sigma}=\mathbf{J}\psi^{0,\sigma}
. The space
\mathscr{N}^{m}_{+}
was introduced in Def.
10.31
and
(\mathscr{N}^{m}_{+})^{\mathrm{c}}
denotes its closure in
\mathscr{N}^{m}
.

Proof.

By Lemma
11.9
without loss of generality we can assume that
V^{2}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}_{+}
,
V^{4}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}_{+}
. The conditions (
11.10
) follow from the charge conjugation invariance. It is clear that the equalities listed in the statement of the lemma hold for some distributions
U^{2},U^{2}_{\partial},U^{4}
such that: (1) they satisfy all of the conditions stated in Def.
11.1
possibly with the exception of the equalities (
11.2
) and (
11.4
), (2) are proportional to the Dirac measures on the diagonal, (3) are invariant under the symmetries of the torus and the internal rotations and (4) are compatible with the flow of the charge. By Remark
10.30
the conditions (2-4) imply that the equalities (
11.2
) and (
11.4
) hold up to a constant. To conclude it is enough to use the properties stated in Remark
11.4
.
∎

Definition 11.12
.

Let

V^{2}=(V^{2,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}\subset\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{4})^{\mathbb{A}^{2}\times\mathbb{G}^{2}}

be such that
\mathcal{X}V^{2}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}
for all translationally invariant polynomials
\mathcal{X}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{4})
of degree two. We define
\hat{W}^{2}\in\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{2}\subset\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{4})^{\mathbb{A}^{2}\times\mathbb{G}^{2}}
and
\check{W}^{2}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{4})^{\mathbb{A}^{2}\times\mathbb{A}^{2}\times\mathbb{G}^{2}}
by Eq. (
10.7
) with

\hat{W}_{\mathrm{K}}^{2,a,\sigma}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2}):=\frac{1}{a!}(x_{1}-x_{1})^{a_{1}}(x_{2}-x_{1})^{a_{2}}\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\,u^{-2}\,(V_{\mathrm{K}}^{2,0,\sigma})(u^{-1}x_{1},u^{-1}\mathrm{d}x_{2})\,\mathrm{d}u

(11.15)

and

\check{W}_{\mathrm{K}}^{2,b,c,\sigma}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2}):=(x_{1}-x_{1})^{b_{1}}(x_{2}-x_{1})^{b_{2}}\int_{0}^{1}u^{-2}\,(V_{\mathrm{K}}^{2,c,\sigma})(u^{-1}x_{1},u^{-1}\mathrm{d}x_{2})\,\mathrm{d}u

(11.16)

if
|a|=2
,
|b|=|c|=1
and
\hat{W}_{\mathrm{K}}^{2,a,\sigma}=0
,
\check{W}_{\mathrm{K}}^{2,b,c,\sigma}=0
otherwise. We define
W^{2}\in\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{2}
by the equality

W^{2,a,\sigma}=\hat{W}^{2,a,\sigma}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b,c\in\mathbb{A}^{2}\\
b+c=a\end{subarray}}\check{W}^{2,b,c,\sigma}.

(11.17)

We define
\mathbf{R}V^{2}\equiv((\mathbf{R}V^{2})^{a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}
by the equalities

\displaystyle(\mathbf{R}V^{2})^{a,\sigma}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\qquad|a|

\displaystyle\leq 1,

(11.18)

\displaystyle(\mathbf{R}V^{2})^{a,\sigma}

\displaystyle=V^{2,a,\sigma}+(\mathbf{S}W^{2})^{a,\sigma},

\displaystyle\qquad|a|

\displaystyle=2,

\displaystyle(\mathbf{R}V^{2})^{a,\sigma}

\displaystyle=V^{2,a,\sigma},

\displaystyle\qquad|a|

\displaystyle\geq 3.

Let

V^{4}=(V^{4,a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{4},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}\subset\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{8})^{\mathbb{A}^{4}\times\mathbb{G}^{4}}

be such that
\mathcal{X}V^{4}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}
for all translationally invariant polynomials
\mathcal{X}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{8})
of degree one. We define
W^{4}\in\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{4}\subset\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{8})^{\mathbb{A}^{4}\times\mathbb{G}^{4}}
by Eq. (
10.7
) with

W_{\mathrm{K}}^{4,a,\sigma}(x_{1},\mathrm{d}x_{2},\mathrm{d}x_{3},\mathrm{d}x_{4}):=(x_{1}-x_{1})^{a_{1}}(x_{2}-x_{1})^{a_{2}}(x_{3}-x_{1})^{a_{3}}(x_{4}-x_{1})^{a_{4}}\\
\times\int_{0}^{1}u^{-6}\,(V_{\mathrm{K}}^{4,0,\sigma})(u^{-1}x_{1},u^{-1}\mathrm{d}x_{2},u^{-1}\mathrm{d}x_{3},u^{-1}\mathrm{d}x_{4})\,\mathrm{d}u

(11.19)

if
|a|=1
and
W_{\mathrm{K}}^{4,a,\sigma}=0
otherwise. We define
\mathbf{R}V^{4}\equiv((\mathbf{R}V^{4})^{a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{4},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}
by the equalities

\displaystyle(\mathbf{R}V^{4})^{a,\sigma}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\qquad|a|

\displaystyle=0,

(11.20)

\displaystyle(\mathbf{R}V^{4})^{a,\sigma}

\displaystyle=V^{4,a,\sigma}+(\mathbf{S}W^{4})^{a,\sigma},

\displaystyle\qquad|a|

\displaystyle=1,

\displaystyle(\mathbf{R}V^{4})^{a,\sigma}

\displaystyle=V^{4,a,\sigma},

\displaystyle\qquad|a|

\displaystyle\geq 2.

Remark 11.13
.

Recall that
\mathbf{S}V
, introduced in Def.
10.4
, denotes the antisymmetric part of
V
. Note that
\hat{W}_{\mathrm{K}}^{2,a,\sigma}=0
unless
a_{1}=0
,
\check{W}_{\mathrm{K}}^{2,b,c,\sigma}=0
unless
b_{1}=0
and
W_{\mathrm{K}}^{4,a,\sigma}=0
unless
a_{1}=0
.

Remark 11.14
.

It follows from Def.
9.1
of the weight
w^{m}_{s}
that if
V^{2}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{2;\gamma}
, then
\mathcal{X}V^{2}_{s}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}
