for all translationally invariant polynomials
\mathcal{X}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{4})
and all
s\in(0,1)
and if
V^{4}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{4;\gamma}
, then
\mathcal{X}V^{4}_{s}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}
for all translationally invariant polynomials
\mathcal{X}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{8})
and all
s\in(0,1)
.

Remark 11.15
.

The map
\mathbf{R}
is compatible with the symmetries. More precisely, let
\tau\in\{0,+\}
and suppose that
V^{2}\in(\mathscr{N}^{2}_{\tau})^{\mathrm{c}}\subset\mathscr{N}^{2}
is such that
\mathcal{X}V^{2}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}
for all translationally invariant polynomials
\mathcal{X}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{4})
of degree two and
V^{4}\in(\mathscr{N}^{4}_{+})^{\mathrm{c}}\subset\mathscr{N}^{4}
is such that
\mathcal{X}V^{4}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}
for all translationally invariant polynomials
\mathcal{X}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{8})
of degree one. Then
\mathbf{R}V^{2}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}_{\tau}
and
\mathbf{R}V^{4}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}_{\tau}
.

Lemma 11.16
.

There exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that it holds

\displaystyle\|w^{2}_{t;\nu}\,(\mathbf{R}V^{2})^{a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\,(1-s/t)^{\zeta_{\star}}\sup_{b\in\mathbb{A}^{2}}s^{|a|-|b|}\,\|w^{2}_{s;\nu}\,V^{2,b,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}},

(11.21)

\displaystyle\|w^{4}_{t;\nu}\,(\mathbf{R}V^{4})^{a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq C\,(1-s/t)^{\zeta_{\star}}\sup_{b\in\mathbb{A}^{4}}s^{|a|-|b|}\,\|w^{4}_{s;\nu}\,V^{4,b,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{4}}.

for all
\nu\in[0,1/2]
,
t\in(0,1]
,
s\in(0,t)
and all
V^{2}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}
,
V^{4}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}
such that the RHS of the above bounds are finite.

Remark 11.17
.

Recall that
\zeta_{\star}=-3/4
was introduced in Def.
9.1
. See also Lemma
9.5
(f).

Proof.

Since by assumption
V^{2},V^{4}
are antisymmetric it suffices to prove that for all
a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}
,
|a|=2
,
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
it holds

\|w^{2}_{t;\nu}\,\hat{W}^{2,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}}\leq C\,(1-s/t)^{\zeta_{\star}}\,s^{|a|}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\|w^{2}_{s;\nu}\,V^{2,0,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}},

(11.22)

for all
b,c\in\mathbb{A}^{2}
,
|b|=|c|=1
,
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
it holds

\|w^{2}_{t;\nu}\,\check{W}^{2,b,c,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}}\leq C\,(1-s/t)^{\zeta_{\star}}\,s^{|b|}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\|w^{2}_{s;\nu}\,V^{2,c,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}}

(11.23)

and for all
a\in\mathbb{A}^{4}
,
|a|=1
,
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}
it holds

\|w^{4}_{t;\nu}\,W^{4,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{4}}\leq C\,(1-s/t)^{\zeta_{\star}}\,s^{|a|}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}}\|w^{4}_{s;\nu}\,V^{4,0,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{4}}.

(11.24)

The above estimates follow easily from Def.
11.12
and Lemma
9.5
(f).
∎

Lemma 11.18
.

Let
V^{2}\in(\mathscr{N}^{2}_{+})^{\mathrm{c}}\subset\mathscr{N}^{2}
be such that
\mathcal{X}V^{2}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}
for all translationally invariant polynomials
\mathcal{X}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{4})
of degree two and
V^{4}\in(\mathscr{N}^{4}_{+})^{\mathrm{c}}\subset\mathscr{N}^{4}
be such that
\mathcal{X}V^{4}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}
for all translationally invariant polynomials
\mathcal{X}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{8})
of degree one. Then it holds

\begin{gathered}\langle(\mathbf{R}+U^{2}\mathbf{L}+U_{\partial}^{2}\mathbf{L}_{\partial})V^{2},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle=\langle V^{2},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle,\\
\langle(\mathbf{R}+U^{4}\mathbf{L})V^{4},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle=\langle V^{4},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle.\end{gathered}

(11.25)

Proof.

By Lemmas
11.9
and
11.16
both sides of the identities listed in the statement of the lemma depend continuously on
V^{2}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}
,
V^{4}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}
. Thus, without loss of generality we can assume that
V^{2}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}_{+}
,
V^{4}\in\mathscr{N}^{4}_{+}
. The rest of the proof is an application of the Taylor theorem. We first note that the equality

\langle((\mathbf{R}+U^{2}\mathbf{L}+U_{\partial}^{2}\mathbf{L}_{\partial})V^{2})^{a,\sigma},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{1},\sigma_{1}}\otimes(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{2},\sigma_{2}}\rangle=\langle V^{2,a,\sigma},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{1},\sigma_{1}}\otimes(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{2},\sigma_{2}}\rangle

(11.26)

holds for all
a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}
,
|a|\geq 2
, and
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
and the equality

\langle((\mathbf{R}+U^{4}\mathbf{L})V^{4})^{a,\sigma},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{1},\sigma_{1}}\otimes\ldots\otimes(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{4},\sigma_{4}}\rangle=\langle V^{4,a,\sigma},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{1},\sigma_{1}}\otimes\ldots\otimes(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{4},\sigma_{4}}\rangle

(11.27)

for all
a\in\mathbb{A}^{4}
,
|a|\geq 1
, and
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}
. In general the above equalities are false but we will prove that the sums of both sides over
a
and
\sigma
coincide. This follows from Def.
11.12
of the map
\mathbf{R}
as well as Eq. (
11.29
), (
11.31
) and (
11.33
) established below. We first observe that by the Taylor theorem the following identity

\psi^{\sigma_{1}}(x_{1})\,\psi^{\sigma_{2}}(x_{2})=\psi^{\sigma_{1}}(x_{1})\,\psi^{\sigma_{2}}(x_{1})+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}\\
|a|=1\end{subarray}}(x_{1}-x_{1})^{a_{1}}(x_{2}-x_{1})^{a_{2}}(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{1},\sigma_{1}}(x_{1})\,(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{2},\sigma_{2}}(x_{1})\\
+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}\\
|a|=2\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{a!}(x_{1}-x_{1})^{a_{1}}(x_{2}-x_{1})^{a_{2}}\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\,(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{1},\sigma_{1}}(x_{1})\,(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{2},\sigma_{2}}(x_{1}+u(x_{2}-x_{1}))\,\mathrm{d}u

(11.28)

is true for
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
. As a result, by Lemma
11.10
and Def.
11.12
of
\hat{W}^{2,a,\sigma}
we obtain

\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\langle V^{2,0,\sigma},\psi^{\sigma_{1}}\otimes\psi^{\sigma_{2}}\rangle\\
=\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\langle U^{2,a,\sigma}\mathbf{L}V^{2}+U_{\partial}^{2,a,\sigma}\hat{\mathbf{L}}_{\partial}V^{2}+\hat{W}^{2,a,\sigma},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{1},\sigma_{1}}\otimes(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{2},\sigma_{2}}\rangle.

(11.29)

Next, we note that

(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{c_{1},\sigma_{1}}(x_{1})\,(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{c_{2},\sigma_{2}}(x_{2})=(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{c_{1},\sigma_{1}}(x_{1})(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{c_{2},\sigma_{2}}(x_{1})\\
+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}b\in\mathbb{A}^{2}\\
|b|=1\end{subarray}}(x_{1}-x_{1})^{b_{1}}(x_{2}-x_{1})^{b_{2}}\int_{0}^{1}(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{b_{1}+c_{1},\sigma_{1}}(x_{1})\,(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{b_{1}+c_{1},\sigma_{2}}(x_{1}+u(x_{2}-x_{1}))\,\mathrm{d}u.

(11.30)

for
c\in\mathbb{A}^{2}
and
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
. Consequently, by Lemma
11.10
and Def.
11.12
of
\check{W}^{2,b,c,\sigma}
we have

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c\in\mathbb{A}^{2}\\
|c|=1\end{subarray}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\langle V^{2,c,\sigma},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{c_{1},\sigma_{1}}\otimes(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{c_{2},\sigma_{2}}\rangle=\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\langle U_{\partial}^{2,a,\sigma}\check{\mathbf{L}}_{\partial}V^{2},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{1},\sigma_{1}}\otimes(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{2},\sigma_{2}}\rangle\\
+\sum_{b,c\in\mathbb{A}^{2}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\langle\check{W}^{2,b,c,\sigma},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{b_{1}+c_{1},\sigma_{1}}\otimes(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{b_{2}+c_{2},\sigma_{2}}\rangle.

(11.31)

Finally, using the identity

\prod_{j=1}^{4}\psi^{\sigma_{j}}(x_{j})=\prod_{j=1}^{4}\psi^{\sigma_{j}}(x_{1})+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}a\in\mathbb{A}^{4}\\
|a|=1\end{subarray}}\prod_{j=1}^{4}(x_{j}-x_{1})^{a_{j}}\int_{0}^{1}\,\prod_{j=1}^{4}(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{j},\sigma_{j}}(x_{1}+u(x_{j}-x_{1}))\,\mathrm{d}u,

(11.32)

Lemma
11.10
and Def.
11.12
of
W^{4,a,\sigma}
we get

\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}}\langle V^{4,0,\sigma},\psi^{\sigma_{1}}\otimes\ldots\otimes\psi^{\sigma_{4}}\rangle\\
=\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{4}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{4}}\langle U^{4,a,\sigma}\mathbf{L}V^{4}+W^{4,a,\sigma},(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{1},\sigma_{1}}\otimes\ldots\otimes(\mathbf{J}\psi)^{a_{4},\sigma_{4}}\rangle.

(11.33)

This finishes the proof.
∎

12
Useful maps

In this section we study properties of the operators that appear on the RHS of the equations (
2.38
), (
2.48
),
(
2.32
), (
2.33
),
(
2.34
) introduced in Sec.
2
. Recall that our goal is to solve this system of equation by rewriting it as a fixed point equation of a certain map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
, which is defined in Sec.
13
. The estimates we establish below play a crucial role in the proof that the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
is well-defined and is a contraction.

Definition 12.1
.

For
\tau,\varepsilon\in[1,0]
and
t\in(0,1]
we define

\dot{H}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\equiv(\dot{H}^{a,\sigma}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}^{2}\times\mathbb{G}^{2}}

by the formula

\dot{H}^{a,\sigma}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(x,y):=\partial^{a_{1}}_{x}\partial^{a_{2}}_{y}\dot{G}^{\sigma}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(x-y)

(12.1)

for all
a=(a_{1},a_{2})\in\mathbb{A}^{2}=\{0,1,2\}^{2}
,
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
and
x,y\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
. We omit
\tau
if
\tau=0
and we omit
\varepsilon
if
\varepsilon=0
.

Remark 12.2
.

The function
\dot{H}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}^{2}\times\mathbb{G}^{2}}
is antisymmetric and invariant under the symmetries of the torus, the internal symmetries and the charge conjugation symmetry. The proof of the invariance under the symmetries of the torus and the internal symmetries is straightforward. To prove the invariance under the charge conjugation symmetry one uses Remark
4.3
. If
\tau=0
, then the above function is also invariant under the symmetries of the plane.

Lemma 12.3
.

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
,
\varepsilon\in[1,0]
,
t\in(0,1]
, and
a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}
,
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}
it holds

\|w_{t}^{2}\dot{H}^{a,\sigma}_{\varepsilon;t}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}}\leq\lambda^{-\kappa}\,t^{-|a|},\qquad\|w_{t}^{2}(\dot{H}^{a,\sigma}_{t}-\dot{H}^{a,\sigma}_{\varepsilon;t})\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}}\leq\lambda^{-\kappa}\,\lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\lambda_{t}^{-\kappa}\,t^{-|a|},

(12.2)

where the weight
w^{2}_{t}
was introduced in Def.
9.1
. Moreover, the map
(0,1]\ni t\mapsto\dot{H}_{\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}
is continuous for all
\varepsilon\in[0,1]
.

Proof.

Observe that
w^{2}_{t}(x_{1},x_{2})=(1+|x_{1}-x_{2}|)^{1/2}\exp(t^{-\zeta}|x_{1}-x_{2}|^{\zeta})
and
\zeta=4/5<\zeta_{\flat}
. As a result, by Lemma
5.3
and Remark
5.2
(E) there exists a universal constant
C\in(0,\infty)
such that
\|w_{t}^{2}\dot{H}^{a,\sigma}_{\varepsilon;t}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}}\leq C\,t^{-|a|}
. This proves the first of the estimates stated in the lemma. The second of the estimates follows from the above bound and Remark
5.2
(C).
∎

Definition 12.4
.

Let
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
,
\varepsilon\in[0,1]
,
s\in(0,1]
. The map
\mathbf{B}^{m}_{\varepsilon;s}\,:\,\mathscr{N}(\mathscr{F})\to\mathscr{N}^{m}(\mathscr{F})
is defined by

\langle\mathbf{B}^{m}_{\varepsilon;s}(V),\varphi^{\otimes m}\rangle:=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k=0}^{m}(-1)^{m-k}\,(k+1)(m-k+1)\,\langle V^{k+1}\otimes V^{m-k+1},\varphi^{\otimes k}\otimes\dot{H}_{\varepsilon;s}\otimes\varphi^{\otimes(m-k)}\rangle

(12.3)

for all
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
. The map
\mathbf{B}^{m}_{\varepsilon;s}\,:\,\mathscr{N}(\mathscr{F})\times\mathscr{N}(\mathscr{F})\to\mathscr{N}^{m}(\mathscr{F})
is defined by

\mathbf{B}^{m}_{\varepsilon;s}(V,W):=(\mathbf{B}^{m}_{\varepsilon;s}(V+W,V+W)-\mathbf{B}^{m}_{\varepsilon;s}(V-W,V-W))/4.

(12.4)

The maps
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}\,:\,\mathscr{N}\to\mathscr{N}
,
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}\,:\,\mathscr{N}\times\mathscr{N}\to\mathscr{N}
are uniquely defined by the condition
\Pi^{m}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}=\mathbf{B}^{m}_{\varepsilon;s}
for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. We also set
\mathbf{B}^{m,a}_{\varepsilon;s}:=\Pi^{m,a}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}
and
\mathbf{B}^{m,a,\sigma}_{\varepsilon;s}:=\Pi^{m,a,\sigma}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}
. We omit
\varepsilon
if
\varepsilon=0
.

Remark 12.5
.

Using the antisymmetric property one shows that for
V,W\in\mathscr{N}
it holds

\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V,W)[\varphi]=\langle\mathrm{D}_{\varphi}V[\varphi]\otimes\mathrm{D}_{\varphi}W[\varphi],\dot{H}_{\varepsilon;s}\rangle-\langle\mathrm{D}_{\varphi}V[0]\otimes\mathrm{D}_{\varphi}W[0],\dot{H}_{\varepsilon;s}\rangle.

(12.5)

The above identity implies that

\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V,W)[\varphi+\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}]-\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V,W)[\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}]\\
=\langle\mathrm{D}_{\varphi}V[\varphi+\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}]\otimes\mathrm{D}_{\varphi}W[\varphi+\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}],\dot{H}_{\varepsilon;s}\rangle\\
-\langle\mathrm{D}_{\varphi}V[\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}]\otimes\mathrm{D}_{\varphi}W[\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}],\dot{H}_{\varepsilon;s}\rangle.

(12.6)

Taking into account Def.
10.22
of the map
\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
we obtain

\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V,W)=\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V,\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}W).

(12.7)

Lemma 12.6
.

Let
\alpha\in[1,\infty)
,
\beta\in(2,\infty)
,
\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}\in[0,\infty)
. There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
,
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{1}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
,
W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{2}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
the map
s\mapsto s\,\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s})
belongs to
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta-1;\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and for all
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{1}}
,
W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{2}}
it holds

(A)

\|s\mapsto s\,\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta-1;\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{1}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\,\|W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{2}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(B)

\|s\mapsto s\,\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s})\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha,\beta-1;\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{1}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\,\|W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{2}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(C)

\|s\mapsto s\,(\mathbf{B}_{s}(V_{s},W_{s})-\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha/2,\beta-1;\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{1}}}\,\|W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{2}}}
.

Proof.

Let
\nu\in\{0,1/2\}
,
s\in(0,1]
and
V_{s},W_{s}\in\mathscr{N}(\mathscr{F})
. By Def.
12.4
of the map
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}
, Def.
10.6
of the norm
\mathscr{M}^{m}
, Lemma
9.5
(a), (b) and Remark
10.17
we obtain

\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|w^{m}_{s;\nu}\,\mathbf{B}^{m,a,\sigma}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s})\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\leq\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\|w^{2}_{s}\dot{H}^{a,\sigma}_{\varepsilon;s}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}}\\
\times\sum_{k=0}^{m}(k+1)(m-k+1)\,\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{k+1}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{k+1}}\|w^{k+1}_{s;\nu}V^{k+1,a,\sigma}_{s}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{k+1}}\,\\
\times\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m-k+1}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m-k+1}}\|w^{m-k+1}_{s}W^{m-k+1,a,\sigma}_{s}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m-k+1}}

(12.8)

and

\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|w^{m}_{s;\nu}\,(\mathbf{B}^{m,a,\sigma}_{s}(V_{s},W_{s})-\mathbf{B}^{m,a,\sigma}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s}))\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\leq\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{2}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{2}}\|w^{2}_{s}(\dot{H}^{a,\sigma}_{s}-\dot{H}^{a,\sigma}_{\varepsilon;s})\|_{\mathscr{M}^{2}}\\
\times\sum_{k=0}^{m}(k+1)(m-k+1)\,\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{k+1}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{k+1}}\|w^{k+1}_{s;\nu}V^{k+1,a,\sigma}_{s}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{k+1}}\,\\
\times\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m-k+1}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m-k+1}}\|w^{m-k+1}_{s}W^{m-k+1,a,\sigma}_{s}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m-k+1}}.

(12.9)

Note that

\rho_{\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2},\kappa}(m)=\rho_{\gamma_{1},\kappa}(k+1)+\rho_{\gamma_{2},\kappa}(m-k+1)-4\kappa.

(12.10)

Moreover, observe that for all
\beta\in(2,\infty)
there exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
such that for all
\alpha\in[1,\infty)
,
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
it holds

\sum_{k=0}^{m}\frac{\alpha^{-(k+1)}}{(k+1)^{\beta-1}}\frac{\alpha^{-(m-k+1)}}{(m-k+1)^{\beta-1}}\leq\lambda^{-\kappa}\,\frac{\alpha^{-m}}{m^{\beta-1}}.

(12.11)

Recall also that
\lambda_{s}^{\kappa}\leq\lambda^{\kappa}
for
s\in(0,1]
. Consequently, by Def.
10.15
of the norm
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
and Lemma
12.3
the bounds (
12.8
) and (
12.9
) imply that

(A
0
)

\|s\mapsto s\,\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta-1;\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}}\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{1}}}\,\|W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{2}}}
,

(B
0
)

\|s\mapsto s\,\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s})\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta-1;\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}}\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{1}}}\,\|W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{2}}}
,

(C
0
)

\|s\mapsto s\,(\mathbf{B}_{s}(V_{s},W_{s})-\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta-1;\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}}\leq\lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{1}}}\,\|W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma_{2}}}
.

The bounds (A) and (B) follow from Def.
10.32
of the norm
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
, the bounds (A
0
) and (B
0
) and the identity (
12.7
). The bound (C) is a consequence of the bound (C
0
) and Lemma
10.26
(C).

It remains to prove that the map
s\mapsto s\,\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s})
belongs to
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta-1;\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
. By the bound (A) and the fact that
\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma}_{\tau}
is a dense subset of
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
it suffices to establish the above claim for
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma_{1}}_{\tau}
and
W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma_{2}}_{\tau}
. It follows from Lemma
12.8
that
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s})\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}_{\tau}
for all
s\in(0,1]
. Recall that the map
(0,1]\ni s\mapsto\dot{H}_{\varepsilon;s}\in\mathscr{N}^{2}
is continuous by Lemma
12.3
. Hence, by a bound similar to the bound (
12.8
) the map
(0,1]\ni s\mapsto\mathbf{B}^{m}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s})\in\mathscr{N}^{m}
is continuous. Using the above fact and the bound (A) we infer that the map
s\mapsto s\,\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s},W_{s})
belongs to
\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}_{\tau}\subset\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta-1;\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
. This finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma 12.7
.

Let
\alpha\in[2,\infty)
,
\beta\in[1,\infty)
,
\gamma\in[0,\infty)
. For
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
,
u\in(0,1]
and
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
we define
\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\equiv(\mathbf{I}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\in\mathscr{N}
by the equalities
\mathbf{I}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}=0
for
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus 2\mathbb{N}_{+}
,

\mathbf{I}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}:=\int_{0}^{u}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u,s}V_{s}/s~\mathrm{d}s\in\mathscr{N}^{m}

(12.12)

for
m\in\{6,8,\ldots\}
and

\mathbf{I}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}:=\int_{0}^{u}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u,s}V_{s}/s~\mathrm{d}s\in\mathscr{N}^{m}

(12.13)

for
m\in\{2,4\}
provided the integrands appearing above are absolutely integrable. We set
\mathbf{I}^{m,a,\sigma}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V=\Pi^{m,a,\sigma}\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V
. Moreover, we omit
\tau
and
\varepsilon
if
\tau=0
and
\varepsilon=0
. There exists a constant
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
the map
u\mapsto\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}
belongs to
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta+1;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
it holds

(A)

\|u\mapsto\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta+1;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(B)

\|u\mapsto\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha,\beta+1;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(C)

\|u\mapsto(\mathbf{I}_{u}-\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u})V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha/4,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
.

Proof.

with

\|u\mapsto\Pi_{>4}(\mathbf{I}_{u}-\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u})V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha/4,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
.

\leq\sup_{b\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\int_{0}^{u}s^{|a|-|b|-1}\,(1-s/u)^{\zeta_{\star}}\,\|w_{s}^{m}\mathbf{A}^{m,b,\sigma}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u,s}V_{s}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\,\mathrm{d}s.

m\in\{5,6,\ldots\}
, which implies that

\|u\mapsto\Pi_{>4}(\mathbf{I}_{u}-\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u})V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha/2,\beta;\gamma}}\leq C\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}.

(12.22)

This finishes the proof of the bound (C
4
).

It remains to prove that the map
u\mapsto\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}
belongs to
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta+1;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
. By the bound (A) and the fact that
\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma}_{\tau}
is a dense subset of
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
it suffices to establish the above claim for
V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma}_{\tau}
. It follows from Lemma
12.8
that
\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}_{\tau}
for all
u\in(0,1]
. By the bound (A) the map
u\mapsto\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;u}V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}
belongs to
\mathscr{V}^{\mathrm{fin};\gamma}_{\tau}\subset\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta+1;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
. This finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma 12.8
.

Let
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t,s\in(0,1]
. If
V\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}_{\tau}
, then
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;t}(V)\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}_{\tau}
and
\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}_{\tau}
.

Proof.

We have to prove that if
V\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}
is invariant under the symmetries of the torus/plane and the internal symmetries and compatible with the flow of charge, then
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;t}(V)
and
\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V
have the same properties. First, note that the invariance of
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;t}(V)
and
\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V
under the symmetries of the torus/plane and the internal symmetries is a simple consequence of Def.
10.22
and
12.4
of the maps
\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
and
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;t}
as well as Remarks
12.2
and
6.14
. The non-trivial part of the proof is the verification of the compatibility with the flow of charge of
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;t}(V)
and
\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V
. For an argument based on the analysis of Feynman diagrams we refer the reader to
[
GK85a
, Sec. 5.B]
. Let us give a direct proof of the above claim. Since by assumption
V\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}
is compatible with the flow of charge for every
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
there exists
W^{m}\in\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{m}
such that
V^{m}=\mathbf{S}W^{m}
and
W^{m}
satisfies the properties listed in Def.
10.28
. Our goal is to prove that:

(1)

for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
there exists
U^{m}\in\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{m}
such that
\mathbf{B}^{m}_{\varepsilon;t}V=\mathbf{S}U^{m}
and
U^{m}
satisfies the conditions listed in Def.
10.28
and

(2)

for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
there exists
U^{m}\in\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{m}
such that
\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V=\mathbf{S}U^{m}
and
U^{m}
satisfies the conditions listed in Def.
10.28
.

To prove (1) is enough to observe that
U^{m}:=\mathbf{B}^{m}_{\varepsilon;t}(W)
satisfies the above-mentioned conditions by Remark
12.2
. The proof of (2) is more involved. Let us set
\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{-1}=\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{0}=\mathbb{C}
. For
n\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
i,j\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
such that
i<j
we define the operators

\mathbf{D}^{(i,j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s},\,\mathbf{D}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s},\,\tilde{\mathbf{D}}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\,:\,\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{n}\to\mathscr{N}_{\sharp}^{n-2}

(12.23)

by the equalities
\mathbf{D}^{(i,j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V=0
for
j>n
,

\langle\mathbf{D}^{(i,j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V,\varphi_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{n}\rangle\\
:=\mathbf{E}\langle V,\varphi_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{i-1}\otimes\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}\otimes\varphi_{i+1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{j-1}\otimes\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s,s}\otimes\varphi_{j+1}\ldots\otimes\varphi_{n}\rangle

(12.24)

for
j\leq n
and

\mathbf{D}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}:=2\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\sum_{j=i+1}^{\infty}\mathbf{D}^{(i,j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s},\qquad\tilde{\mathbf{D}}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}:=(n\mathbf{D}^{(1,2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}+n(n-2)\mathbf{D}^{(2,3)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}).

(12.25)

Note that by Remark
6.14
and Def.
12.1
the expected value appearing in the equation defining
\mathbf{D}^{(i,j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
can be expressed in terms of the function
H_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}-H_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s}
. We observe that for all
V\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}
and
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
it holds

\begin{gathered}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V=\exp(\mathbf{D}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}/2)V=\exp(\tilde{\mathbf{D}}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}/2)V,\\
\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{n!}(\mathbf{D}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}/2)^{n}\,V^{m+2n}=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{n!}(\tilde{\mathbf{D}}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}/2)^{n}\,V^{m+2n},\end{gathered}

(12.26)

where we used the fact that
V^{m}
is antisymmetric for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. The series on the RHS of the second equation above are actually finite sums due to the assumption
V\in\mathscr{N}^{\mathrm{fin}}
. For
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
we define

U^{m}:=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{n!}(\tilde{\mathbf{D}}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}/2)^{n}\,W^{m+2n}.

(12.27)

Recall that
(W^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}
satisfies the properties (a), (b), (c) stated in Def.
10.28
. Using the properties (b) and (c) we prove that
\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}V=\mathbf{S}U^{m}
for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. Using the property (a) of
(W^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}
and the invariance of
H_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}-H_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s}
under the charge conjugation symmetry we show the property (a) of
(U^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}
. The properties (b) and (c) of
(U^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}
follow from the properties (b) and (c) of
(W^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}
and the fact that
H_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}-H_{\tau,\varepsilon;t\vee s}
is antisymmetric. This finishes the proof.
∎

13
Fixed point problem

In this section we construct a solution
X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\equiv(g_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},W_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})
of the system of equations (
2.38
), (
2.48
),
(
2.32
), (
2.33
),
(
2.34
) introduced in Sec.
2
. To this end, we rewrite this system of equations as a fixed point equation of a certain map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
acting in a complete metric space
\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and prove that the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
is a contraction provided the parameter
\lambda\in(0,1]
is sufficiently small. As argued in Sec.
2
and proved in Sec.
14
using
X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
it is possible to construct a solution
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
of the flow equation (
7.13
).

Definition 13.1
.

For
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and

X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in C((0,1],\mathbb{C})\times C((0,1],\mathbb{C})\times C((0,1],\mathbb{C})\times\mathscr{W}^{8,4;2-80\kappa}_{\tau,\varepsilon}

(13.1)

we define

\begin{gathered}\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{P}}:=\sup_{t\in(0,1]}\lambda_{t}^{1+10\kappa}\,|g_{t}|+\sup_{t\in(0,1]}\lambda_{t}^{36\kappa-1}\,t\,|r_{t}|+\sup_{t\in(0,1]}\lambda_{t}^{36\kappa-1}\,|z_{t}|,\\
\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip (13.2)

By definition the vector space
\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
over
\mathbb{C}
consists of maps
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}
of the above form such that
\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}<\infty
. We also define the set

\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}:=\{X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\,|\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq 1,~\mathscr{P}_{\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\geq\lambda^{\kappa},~\forall_{t\in(0,1]}\,\mathrm{Im}\,g_{t}=\mathrm{Im}\,r_{t}=\mathrm{Im}\,z_{t}=0\},

(13.3)

where
\mathscr{P}_{\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=\inf_{t\in(0,1]}\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee t}\,g_{t}
.
We omit
\tau,\varepsilon
if
\tau=0
,
\varepsilon=0
.

Definition 13.2
.

For
\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in(0,1]
we define
\theta_{\varepsilon;t}:=\lambda_{t}/\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee t}\in[0,1]
.

Lemma 13.3
.

For all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
the space
(\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon},\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}})
is a Banach space. Moreover, the set
\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
is a closed subset of
\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
.

Proof.

The statement follows from Lemma
10.37
.
∎

Lemma 13.4
.

Let
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
.

(A)

For all
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
it holds
\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
.

(B)

For all
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{X}
it holds
\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}}
.

Proof.

Item (A) follows from Def.
10.32
and Remark
10.18
. To prove Item (B) observe that

\|W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,3;2-80\kappa}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\|W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{2,3;2-80\kappa}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\|W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{4,3;2-80\kappa}}\leq\|W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{8,4;2-80\kappa}}\leq\|W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;2-80\kappa}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}

(13.4)

by Remark
10.33
,
10.35
,
10.18
and
10.34
, respectively. ∎

Definition 13.5
.

Let
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
. For
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\equiv(g_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},r_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and
t\in(0,1]
define

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s\in\mathscr{N}^{m},

\displaystyle m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus\{2,4\},

(13.5)

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s-\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{m}W_{t}\in\mathscr{N}^{m},\qquad

\displaystyle m\in\{2,4\},

as well as

\displaystyle\mathbf{g}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=

\displaystyle\lambda^{-1}+\int_{t}^{1}(g_{s})^{2}\,\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s\in\mathbb{R},

(13.6)

\displaystyle\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=

\displaystyle-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1/g_{t},0,0)-\int_{t}^{1}\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s\in\mathbb{R},

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{L}_{\partial}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s\in\mathbb{R},

where

V_{s}\equiv V_{s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=U(1/g_{s},r_{s},z_{s})+W_{s}\in\mathscr{N},\qquad s\in(0,1].

(13.7)

For
\lambda\in(0,1]
small enough the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\to\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
is defined by

\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}:=\mathbf{g}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\times\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},\qquad\mathbf{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip
