(13.8)

Remark 13.6
.

The fact that the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
is well-defined is non-trivial and is a consequence of the estimates established below. We also point out that
\mathbf{W}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=0
for
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus 2\mathbb{N}_{+}
by Remark
8.7
.

Remark 13.7
.

We call the parameter
g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
the inverse of the effective coupling constant. Note that we fixed
g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
at unit scale
t=1
to be
g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t=1}:=\lambda^{-1}
, where
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
and
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
is a small constant.

Remark 13.8
.

The parameter
r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
is the correction to the effective mass due to the interaction. Note that we fixed
r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
at unit scale
t=1
to be
r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t=1}=0
. Recall that that the free part of the action contains the unit mass term. Hence, the above condition implies that the effective mass at unit scale equals one.

Remark 13.9
.

The fact that
\mathbf{g}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}),\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}),\mathbf{z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathbb{R}
follows from Lemma
11.10
and the symmetry properties of functionals
U(g,r,z)
and
W\in\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
.

Theorem 13.10
.

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and all
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
,
Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}
it holds:

(A)

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}
and
\mathscr{P}_{\varepsilon}(s\mapsto\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\geq 1/C
,

(B)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(C)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(D)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{X}_{s}(Z_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})-\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Z_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}
.

Proof.

The theorem is an immediate consequence of Lemmas
13.18
,
13.26
,
13.27
,
13.28
.
∎

Corollary 13.11
.

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
and all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\to\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
is well-defined and has the unique fixed point denoted by
X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\equiv(g_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},W_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})
such that

\|X_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}-X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,,

(13.9)

where
X_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}:=X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
with
\tau=0
,
\varepsilon=0
, and

\|V_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{8,4;1-40\kappa}}\leq C\,,\qquad\|V_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}-V_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{2,3;1-40\kappa}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,

(13.10)

where

V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\equiv(V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}:=U(\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;t}/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;1},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})+W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{N}

(13.11)

for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in(0,1]
, we set
V_{t}:=V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
with
\tau=0
,
\varepsilon=0
, the norms
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{N}^{m}}
,
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}
were introduced in Def.
10.12
and the function
\theta_{\varepsilon;t}
was introduced in Def.
13.2
.

Proof.

The fact that for all sufficiently small
\lambda\in(0,1]
the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\to\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
is well-defined and is a contraction follows from Theorem
13.10
and Remark
13.9
. The existence and uniqueness of the fixed point
X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
is then a consequence of Lemma
13.3
and the Banach fixed point theorem. In order to prove the bound (
13.9
) note that

X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}=\mathbf{X}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}(X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})\,.

(13.12)

Consequently, by the triangle inequality we obtain

\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\|\mathbf{X}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}(X_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})-\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}(X_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}+\|\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}(X_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})-\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}(X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\,.

(13.13)

Since
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}
,
X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
by the bounds (C) and (D) stated in Theorem

it holds

\|\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}

(13.14)

and

\|\mathbf{X}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}(X_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})-\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}(X_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,.

(13.15)

Consequently, we have

\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}+C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}.

(13.16)

This proves the bound (
13.9
). Next, note that

V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=\mathbf{V}^{(0)}_{\varepsilon;t}(X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}),\qquad V_{t}-V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=\mathbf{V}^{(0)}_{t}(X_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})-\mathbf{V}^{(0)}_{\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}(X_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})+\mathbf{V}^{(0)}_{\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}(X_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})-\mathbf{V}^{(0)}_{\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}(X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})

(13.17)

where the map
\mathbf{V}^{(0)}_{\varepsilon;t}
was defined in Lemma
13.15
. Hence, the bounds (
13.10
) are immediate consequences of Lemma
13.15
. This finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma 13.12
.

For all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and

X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon},\qquad Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\equiv(\tilde{g}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\tilde{r}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\tilde{z}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon},\qquad Z\equiv(\hat{g}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\hat{r}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\hat{z}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\hat{W}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathscr{Y}

(13.18)

it holds

(A)

\sup_{s\in(0,1]}\lambda^{12\kappa-1}_{s}\,|\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/g_{s}|\leq 1
,

(B)

\sup_{s\in(0,1]}\lambda^{12\kappa-1}_{s}\,|\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/g_{s}-\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/\tilde{g}_{s}|\leq\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(C)

\sup_{s\in(0,1]}\lambda^{12\kappa-1}_{s}\,|\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/g_{s}-\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/\hat{g}_{s}|\leq\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(D)

\sup_{s\in(0,1]}\lambda^{12\kappa-1}_{s}\,|1/\hat{g}_{s}-\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/\hat{g}_{s}|\leq\lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\kappa}
,

where
\theta_{\varepsilon;t}
was introduced in Def.
13.2
.

Proof.

First observe that

\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee s}\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}\leq\lambda_{s}
,

\lambda^{2}_{\varepsilon\vee s}\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}\leq\lambda^{2}_{s}
and
\lambda_{s}\leq\lambda
.
The above bounds and Def.
13.1
imply that

(A
1
)

\sup_{s\in(0,1]}\lambda^{\kappa-1}_{s}\,|\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/g_{s}|\leq\lambda_{s}^{\kappa}/\mathscr{P}_{\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})
,

(B
1
)

\sup_{s\in(0,1]}\lambda^{12\kappa-1}_{s}\,|\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/g_{s}-\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/\tilde{g}_{s}|\leq\lambda_{s}^{\kappa}/\mathscr{P}_{\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})~\lambda_{s}^{\kappa}/\mathscr{P}_{\varepsilon}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})~\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{P}}
,

(C
1
)

\sup_{s\in(0,1]}\lambda^{12\kappa-1}_{s}\,|\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/g_{s}-\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/\hat{g}_{s}|\leq\lambda_{s}^{\kappa}/\mathscr{P}_{\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})~\lambda_{s}^{\kappa}/\mathscr{P}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})~\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{P}}
,

(D
1
)

\sup_{s\in(0,1]}\lambda^{2\kappa-1}_{s}\,|1/\hat{g}_{s}-\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/\hat{g}_{s}|\leq\lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\lambda_{s}^{\kappa}/\mathscr{P}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})
.

The lemma follows now from Def.
13.1
.
∎

Lemma 13.13
.

Let
\alpha,\beta\in[1,\infty)
,
\gamma\in[0,\infty)
. There exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
it holds:

(A)

\|s\mapsto\lambda_{s}^{-\gamma-8\kappa}\,U(1,0,0)\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C
,

(B)

\|s\mapsto\lambda_{s}^{-\gamma-4\kappa}\,s\,U(0,1,0)\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C
,

(C)

\|s\mapsto\lambda_{s}^{-\gamma-4\kappa}\,U(0,0,1)\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C
.

Moreover, analogous bounds with
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
replaced by
\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
are true.

Proof.

From Def.
11.1
of the functional
U(g,r,z)
and Def.
10.15
of the norms
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
and
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
it follows that the bounds

(A
1
)

\|s\mapsto\lambda_{s}^{-\gamma-8\kappa}\,U(1,0,0)\|_{\mathscr{V}^{2\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}\leq C
,

(B
1
)

\|s\mapsto\lambda_{s}^{-\gamma-4\kappa}\,s\,U(0,1,0)\|_{\mathscr{V}^{2\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}\leq C
,

(C
1
)

\|s\mapsto\lambda_{s}^{-\gamma-4\kappa}\,U(0,0,1)\|_{\mathscr{V}^{2\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}\leq C

as well as the bounds with
\mathscr{V}^{2\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
replaced by
\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{2\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
hold true. The bounds (A), (B), (C) are consequences of the bounds (A
1
), (B
1
), (C
1
) and Remark
10.35
.
∎

Remark 13.14
.

It follows immediately from Def.
10.15
of the norm
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}
and Def.
10.32
of the norm
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
that for all continuous functions
h_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\,:\,(0,1]\to\mathbb{R}
it holds

\|s\mapsto h_{s}\,V_{s}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}}\leq\|h_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\infty}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}},\qquad\|s\mapsto h_{s}\,V_{s}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\|h_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\infty}\,\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}},

(13.19)

where
\|h_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\infty}:=\sup_{s\in(0,1]}|h_{s}|
. The analogous bounds with
\mathscr{V}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
replaced by
\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
and
\mathscr{W}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
replaced by
\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
hold true as well.

Lemma 13.15
.

For
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
,
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\equiv(g_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},r_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and
s\in(0,1]
define

\mathbf{V}^{(1)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=U(0,r_{s},z_{s}),\qquad\mathbf{V}^{(2)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=U(\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/g_{s},0,0),\qquad\mathbf{V}^{(3)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=W_{s}

(13.20)

and

\mathbf{V}^{(0)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=\mathbf{V}^{(1)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})+\mathbf{V}^{(2)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})+\mathbf{V}^{(3)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=U(\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}/g_{s},r_{s},z_{s})+W_{s}.

(13.21)

We omit
\varepsilon
if
\varepsilon=0
. There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
, all
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
,
Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}
and all
i\in\{1,2,3\}
it holds:

(A)

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C
,

(B)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(C)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,3;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(D)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,3;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\kappa}
,

where
\gamma(0)=\gamma(1)=1-40\kappa
,
\gamma(2)=1-20\kappa
and
\gamma(3)=2-80\kappa
.

Proof.

For
i=1
the bounds (A), (B), (C), (D) follow from Lemma
13.13
applied with
\gamma=\gamma(1)
, Remark
13.14
and Def.
13.1
. For
i=2
the bounds (A), (B), (C), (D) follow from Lemma
13.13
applied with
\gamma=\gamma(2)
, Lemma
13.12
, Remark
13.14
and Def.
13.1
. For
i=3
the bounds (A), (B), (C) follow immediately from Def.
13.1
. For
i=0
the bounds (A), (B), (C), (D) are consequences of the bounds (A), (B), (C) with
i\in\{1,2,3\}
and the triangle inequality.
∎

Lemma 13.16
.

For
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
,
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
,
i,j\in\{0,1,2,3\}
and
s\in(0,1]
define

\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}),\mathbf{V}^{(j)}_{s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})),\qquad\mathbf{G}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=\mathbf{G}^{(0,0)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}),

(13.22)

where the maps
\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}
,
i\in\{0,1,2,3\}
, were introduced in Lemma
13.15
. We omit
\varepsilon
if
\varepsilon=0
. There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
, all
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
,
Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}
and all
i,j\in\{0,1,2,3\}
it holds:

(A)

\|s\mapsto s\,\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,3;\gamma(i)+\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}
,

(B)

\|s\mapsto s\,(\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,3;\gamma(i)+\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(C)

\|s\mapsto s\,(\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,2;\gamma(i)+\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(D)

\|s\mapsto s\,(\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,2;\gamma(i)+\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\kappa}
,

where
\gamma(0)=\gamma(1)=1-40\kappa
,
\gamma(2)=1-20\kappa
and
\gamma(3)=2-80\kappa
.

Remark 13.17
.

Observe that for
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip (13.23)

Proof.

Note that since
\dot{H}_{\varepsilon;s}=0
if
s\in(0,\varepsilon]
it holds
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}({\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=0
if
s\in(0,\varepsilon]
. Consequently, since
\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;s}=1
if
s\in(0,\varepsilon]
we obtain

\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}),\mathbf{V}^{(j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))
