(13.27)

The application of Lemma
12.6
with
\gamma_{1}=\gamma(i)
and
\gamma_{2}=\gamma(j)
yields

(A)

\|s\mapsto s\,\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,3;\gamma(i)+\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\,\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(B)

\|s\mapsto s\,(\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,3;\gamma(i)+\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\\
\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\,\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{V}^{(j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{V}^{(j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\\
+\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\,\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(C)

\|s\mapsto s\,(\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,2;\gamma(i)+\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\\
\leq\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{2,3;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\,\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{V}^{(j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{V}^{(j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,3;\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\\
+\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{2,3;\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\,\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,3;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(D)

\|s\mapsto s\,(\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,2;\gamma(i)+\gamma(j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\\
\leq\lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{4,3;\gamma(i)}}\,\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(j)}_{s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{4,3;\gamma(j)}}
.

Next, observe that

\begin{gathered}\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{2,3;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{4,3;\gamma(i)}}\leq\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}},\\
\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{2,3;\gamma(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{4,3;\gamma(i)}}\leq\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;\gamma(i)}},\\
\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{4,3;\gamma(i)}}\leq\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{8,4;\gamma(i)}}\leq\|s\mapsto\mathbf{V}^{(i)}_{\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;\gamma(i)}}\end{gathered}

(13.28)

by Remarks
10.18
,
10.34
and
10.35
. The statement of the lemma is now an immediate consequence of Lemma
13.15
.
∎

Lemma 13.18
.

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and all
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
,
Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}
it holds:

(A)

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;2-80\kappa}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}
,

(B)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,4;2-80\kappa}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(C)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,3;2-80\kappa}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(D)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{W}_{s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,3;2-80\kappa}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}
.

Proof.

For
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
,
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\equiv(g_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},r_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and
s\in(0,1]
we define

\mathbf{H}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=(\mathbf{H}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\in\mathscr{N}

by the equality

\mathbf{H}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=W_{s}-\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}W_{s}=-\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}W_{s}.

(13.29)

Recalling that the maps
\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
and
\mathbf{G}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
were defined in Lemmas
12.7
and
13.16
, respectively, and using the notation introduced in Def.
10.12
we obtain

\mathbf{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})+\mathbf{W}^{(2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}),

(13.30)

where

\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(u\mapsto u\,\mathbf{G}_{\varepsilon;u}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})),\qquad\mathbf{W}^{(2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=(\Pi_{2}+\Pi_{4})\mathbf{H}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}).

(13.31)

Observe that

\begin{gathered}\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(u\mapsto u\,(\mathbf{G}_{\varepsilon;u}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{G}_{\varepsilon;u}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))),\\
\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(u\mapsto u\,(\mathbf{G}_{\varepsilon;u}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{G}_{\varepsilon;u}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))),\\
\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=(\mathbf{I}_{s}-\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})(u\mapsto u\,\mathbf{G}_{u}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))+\mathbf{I}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(u\mapsto u\,(\mathbf{G}_{u}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{G}_{\varepsilon;u}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))).\end{gathered}

(13.32)

It follows now from Lemma
12.7
and Lemma
13.16
that the bounds (A), (B), (C) with
\mathbf{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})
replaced by
\mathbf{W}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})
hold true. Consequently, by the triangle inequality to complete the proof of the lemma it suffices to establish the bounds (A), (B), (C) with
\mathbf{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})
replaced by
\mathbf{W}^{(2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})
. By Remarks
10.35
and
10.19
the latter bounds are implied by the following bounds:

(A
1
)

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{H}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{4,3;2-80\kappa}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}
,

(B
1
)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{H}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{H}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\mathscr{V}^{4,3;2-80\kappa}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(C
1
)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{H}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{H}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{1,2;2-80\kappa}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}
,

(D
1
)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{H}_{s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{H}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{1,2;2-80\kappa}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}
.

The above bounds follow from Lemma
10.26
(B) and (D), Remark

and Def.

. This finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma 13.19
.

Let
\gamma\in[0,\infty)
. For
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
,
W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{8,3;\gamma}
and
s\in(0,1]
define

\displaystyle\mathbf{c}^{m,0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}W_{s},\qquad m\in\{2,4\},

(13.33)

\displaystyle\mathbf{c}^{2,1}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{L}_{\partial}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}W_{s}.

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
,
t\in(0,1]
,
W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\tilde{W}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{8,3;\gamma}
,
\hat{W}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{W}^{8,3;\gamma}
and all
(m,i)\in\{(2,0),(4,0),(2,1)\}
it holds:

(A)

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma-2\kappa m}\,t^{3-m/2-i}\,|\mathbf{c}^{(m,i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\|s\mapsto s\,W_{s}\|_{\mathscr{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{8,3;\gamma}}
,

(B)

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma-2\kappa m}\,t^{3-m/2-i}\,|\mathbf{c}^{(m,i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{c}^{(m,i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(\tilde{W}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\|s\mapsto s\,(W_{s}-\tilde{W}_{s})\|_{\mathscr{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{8,3;\gamma}}
.

(C)

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma-2\kappa m}\,t^{3-m/2-i}\,|\mathbf{c}^{(m,i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{c}^{(m,i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(\hat{W}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\|s\mapsto s\,(W_{s}-\hat{W}_{s})\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{2,2;\gamma}}
,

(D)

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma-2\kappa m}\,t^{3-m/2-i}\,|\mathbf{c}^{(m,i)}_{t}(\hat{W}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{c}^{(m,i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(\hat{W}_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\|s\mapsto s\,\hat{W}_{s}\|_{\mathscr{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{8,3;\gamma}}
.

Remark 13.20
.

Actually, since the map
\mathbf{c}^{(m,i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
is linear Item (B) of the above lemma follows immediately from Item (A).

Proof.

By Remark
10.19
and Lemma
10.26
(A), (C), (E) there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

(A
1
)

\|s\mapsto s\,\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}W_{s}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}\leq C\,\|s\mapsto s\,W_{s}\|_{\mathscr{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{8,3;\gamma}}
,

(B
1
)

\|s\mapsto s\,(\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}W_{s}-\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\tilde{W}_{s})\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}\leq C\,\|s\mapsto s\,(W_{s}-\tilde{W}_{s})\|_{\mathscr{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{8,3;\gamma}}
,

(C
1
)

\|s\mapsto s\,(\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}W_{s}-\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\hat{W}_{s})\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{m;\gamma}}\leq C\,\|s\mapsto s\,(W_{s}-\hat{W}_{s})\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{2,2;\gamma}}
,

(D
1
)

\|s\mapsto s\,(\mathbf{A}^{m}_{1,s}\hat{W}_{s}-\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\hat{W}_{s})\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{m;\gamma}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\|s\mapsto s\,\hat{W}_{s}\|_{\mathscr{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{8,3;\gamma}}

for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
m\in\{2,4\}
. The bounds (A), (B), (C), (D) follow now from Def.
10.15
of the norm
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{m;\gamma}}
, Remark
10.10
and Lemma
11.9
. This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 13.21
.

For
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
,
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\equiv(g_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and

define

\displaystyle\mathbf{a}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})

(13.34)

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(2/g_{s},r_{s},z_{s}),U(0,r_{s},z_{s})),

\displaystyle\mathbf{a}^{(3)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})

\displaystyle\mathbf{a}^{(6)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{L}_{\partial}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(2U(1/g_{s},r_{s},z_{s})+W_{s},W_{s}).

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and

such that for all

, all

, all

, all

,

it holds:

,

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma(i)}\,t\,|\mathbf{a}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{a}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}}
.

,

,

,

Remark 13.22
.

Observe that it holds

\displaystyle(g_{s})^{2}\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s})

\displaystyle=(g_{s})^{2}\mathbf{a}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})+(g_{s})^{2}\mathbf{a}^{(2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})+(g_{s})^{2}\mathbf{a}^{(3)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}),
