. Note that the expressions

(g_{s})^{2}\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s}),\qquad\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s}),\qquad\mathbf{L}_{\partial}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s})

(13.37)

appear in Def.
13.5
of the maps
\mathbf{g}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
,
\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
and
\mathbf{z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
. The second of the above expressions will be estimated directly in Lemma
13.27
using Lemmas
13.19
and
13.16
. One could also estimate directly the remaining two expressions without using the decompositions (
13.36
). However, estimates obtained in that way are not strong enough for our purposes as they do not take into account the fact that
\mathbf{a}^{(2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=0
and
\mathbf{a}^{(6)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=0
for
s\in(0,1/2)
, which we prove below. Let us also mention that we will not use at all the bounds for
\mathbf{a}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})
stated in the above lemma. Instead we will estimate directly
(g_{s})^{2}\mathbf{a}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=f_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
, where
f_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
is introduced in Def.
13.23
. Note that
f_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
does not depend on
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}
.

Proof.

First observe that for
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\equiv(g_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},r_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
it holds

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1/g_{s},0,0))

\displaystyle=\mathbf{G}^{(2,2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}),

(13.38)

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(2/g_{s},r_{s},z_{s}),U(0,r_{s},z_{s}))

\displaystyle=\mathbf{G}^{(1,1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})+2\,\mathbf{G}^{(1,2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}),

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(2U(1/g_{s},r_{s},z_{s})+W_{s},W_{s})

\displaystyle=2\,\mathbf{G}^{(1,3)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})+2\,\mathbf{G}^{(2,3)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})+\mathbf{G}^{(3,3)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}),

where the maps
\mathbf{G}^{(i,j)}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
were introduced in Lemma
13.16
. Using the fact that
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}(x)\,\mathrm{d}x=0
for
s\in(0,1/2)
by Remark
5.2
(D) as well as Def.
10.22
,
12.4
and
11.1
one shows that

\begin{gathered}\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(2/g_{s},r_{s},z_{s}),U(0,r_{s},z_{s}))=0,\\
\mathbf{L}_{\partial}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(2/g_{s},r_{s},z_{s}),U(0,r_{s},z_{s}))=0\end{gathered}

(13.39)

for
s\in(0,1/2)
. Recall that
\theta_{1/2;t}=\lambda_{t}/\lambda_{1/2\vee t}
. Since
\theta_{1/2;s}=1
for
s\in[1/2,1]
we obtain

\mathbf{a}^{(2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=\theta_{1/2;s}^{1-40\kappa}\,\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(2/g_{s},r_{s},z_{s}),U(0,r_{s},z_{s}))

(13.40)

and

\mathbf{a}^{(6)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=\theta_{1/2;s}^{1-40\kappa}\,\mathbf{L}_{\partial}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(2/g_{s},r_{s},z_{s}),U(0,r_{s},z_{s})).

(13.41)

To prove the bounds (A), (B), (C), (D) in the cases
i\in\{1,\ldots,6\}
we use the following arguments, respectively.

(1)

We apply Lemma
13.19
with
m=4
,
i=0
and
\gamma=2-40\kappa
and Lemma
13.16
with
(i,j)=(2,2)
.

(2)

We apply Lemma
13.19
with
m=4
,
i=0
and
\gamma=2-80\kappa
and Lemma
13.16
with
(i,j)\in\{(1,1),(1,2)\}
and use the identity (
13.40
).

(3)

We apply Lemma
13.19
with
m=4
,
i=0
and
\gamma=3-120\kappa
and Lemma
13.16
with
(i,j)\in\{(1,3),(2,3),(3,3)\}
.

(4)

We apply Lemma
13.19
with
m=2
,
i=1
and
\gamma=2-40\kappa
and Lemma
13.16
with
(i,j)=(2,2)
.

(5)

We apply Lemma
13.19
with
m=2
,
i=1
and
\gamma=2-80\kappa
and Lemma
13.16
with
(i,j)\in\{(1,1),(1,2)\}
and use the identity (
13.41
).

(6)

We apply Lemma
13.19
with
m=2
,
i=1
and
\gamma=3-120\kappa
and Lemma
13.16
with
(i,j)\in\{(1,3),(2,3),(3,3)\}
.

This finishes the proof.
∎

Definition 13.23
.

For
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in(0,1]
we define

f_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}:=\lambda^{-1}+\int_{t}^{1}\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))\,\mathrm{d}s,\qquad h_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}:=\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1,0,0).

(13.42)

Remark 13.24
.

For
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\equiv(g_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},r_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
it holds

\mathbf{g}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=f_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}+\int_{t}^{1}(g_{s})^{2}\,(\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s})-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1/g_{s},0,0)))\,\mathrm{d}s

(13.43)

and

\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=-h_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}/g_{t}-\int_{t}^{1}\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\,\mathrm{d}s,

(13.44)

where
V_{s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=U(1/g_{s},r_{s},z_{s})+W_{s}
.

Lemma 13.25
.

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
it holds

(A)

\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee t}^{-1}/C\leq f_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\leq C\,\lambda_{t}^{-1}
,

(B)

|f_{t}-f_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}|\leq C\,\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee\tau}^{\kappa}\,\lambda_{t}^{-1-8\kappa}
,

(C)

|h_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}|\leq C\,\lambda_{t}^{-4\kappa}\,t^{-1}
,

(D)

|h_{t}-h_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}|\leq C\,\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee\tau}^{\kappa}\,\lambda_{t}^{-4\kappa}\,t^{-1}

for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in(0,1]
.

Proof.

Let us first note that
\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s,1}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))
coincides with the RHS of Eq. (
10.29
) with
V=\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))
. Since
\mathbf{B}^{4+k}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))=0
for all
k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
but
k=2
in the case at hand only the term
k=2
contributes to the sum over
k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
on the RHS of Eq. (
10.29
). Consequently, taking into account Eq. (
6.11
) and Def.
12.4
we conclude that
\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))
can be represented in terms of the propagators
H_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
and
\dot{H}_{\varepsilon;s}
introduced in Def.
12.1
, which are defined in terms of
G_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
and
\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}
. More specifically,
\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))
is a linear combination of one-loop Feynman diagrams with two quartic vertices, one of which is integrated over
\mathbb{R}^{2}
. The following types of diagrams appear: (1) diagrams with the vertices connected by two edges representing the propagators
\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}
and
G_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
, respectively, and (2) diagrams with the vertices connected by a single edge representing the propagator
\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}
and with a self-contraction of one of of the vertices by the propagator
G_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
. Note that for
s\in(0,1/2)
the diagrams of the type (2) vanish identically since
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}(x)\,\mathrm{d}x=0
for
s\in(0,1/2)
by Remark
5.2
(D). For
s\in[1/2,1]
both of the above-mentioned diagrams contribute to
\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s,1}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))
. However, in this regime both propagators
\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}
and
G_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
have the UV cutoff of order one and by elementary estimates
\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s,1}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))
is uniformly bounded for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
s\in[1/2,1]
. Thus, we can restrict attention to the diagrams of the type (1) and
s\in(0,1/2]
.

Taking into account all possible contractions of the fields represented by edges of the diagrams of the type (1) we obtain that for
s\in(0,1/2]
and
\tau\in(0,1]
it holds

\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s,1}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))=8(N-1)\,\tau^{2}\!\sum_{p\in(2\pi\tau\mathbb{Z})^{2}}\!\frac{\vartheta(2\varepsilon\omega(p))^{2}\,\vartheta(s\omega(p))\,\dot{\vartheta}(s\omega(p))\,\omega(p)\,(1-|p|^{2})}{(1+|p|^{2})^{2}}

(13.45)

whereas for
s\in(0,1/2]
and
\tau=0
(recall that we omit
\tau
in the notation if
\tau=0
) it holds

\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\varepsilon;s,1}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))=\frac{8(N-1)}{(2\pi)^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\vartheta(2\varepsilon\omega(p))^{2}\,\vartheta(s\omega(p))\,\dot{\vartheta}(s\omega(p))\,\omega(p)\,(1-|p|^{2})}{(1+|p|^{2})^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}p.

(13.46)

As a result, there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

|\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\varepsilon;s,1}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s,1}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))|\leq C\,(1-\log s)

(13.47)

for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
s\in(0,1/2]
. This and the first paragraph of the proof implies that there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

|f_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}-f_{\varepsilon;t}|\leq C

(13.48)

for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in(0,1]
. By the Lebesgue dominated convergence theorem it holds

\lim_{s\searrow 0}s\,\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{s,1}\mathbf{B}_{s}(U(1,0,0))=-\frac{\beta_{2}}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\vartheta(|p|)\,\dot{\vartheta}(|p|)}{|p|}\,\mathrm{d}p=-2\beta_{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta(|p|)\,\dot{\vartheta}(|p|)\,\mathrm{d}|p|=\beta_{2}.

(13.49)

Next, observe that

\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{s,1}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))-\beta_{2}/s\\
=\frac{\beta_{2}}{\pi s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\vartheta((s^{2}+|p|^{2})^{1/2})\,\dot{\vartheta}((s^{2}+|p|^{2})^{1/2})\,(s^{2}-|p|^{2})}{(s^{2}+|p|^{2})^{3/2}}+\frac{\vartheta(|p|)\,\dot{\vartheta}(|p|)}{|p|}\bigg)\,\mathrm{d}p.

(13.50)

Hence, we conclude that there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that it holds

|\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\varepsilon;s,1}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))-\beta_{2}/s|\leq C

(13.51)

for all
\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
s\in(4\varepsilon,1]
. Consequently,

|f_{\varepsilon;t}-\lambda^{-1}_{t}|\leq\int_{t}^{1}|\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))-\beta_{2}/s|\leq C

(13.52)

for all
\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in[4\varepsilon,1]
. Using the bound

|\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\varepsilon;s,1}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))|\\
\leq\frac{\beta_{2}}{\pi s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\vartheta(2\varepsilon(1+|p|^{2}/s^{2})^{1/2})^{2}\,\vartheta((s^{2}+|p|^{2})^{1/2})\,|\dot{\vartheta}((s^{2}+|p|^{2})^{1/2})|\,(s^{2}-|p|^{2})}{(s^{2}+|p|^{2})^{3/2}}\,\mathrm{d}p

(13.53)

we show that there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

|\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\varepsilon;s,1}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))|\leq C/(\varepsilon\vee s)

(13.54)

for all
\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
s\in(0,1]
. Hence,

|f_{\varepsilon;t}-f_{\varepsilon;4\varepsilon}|\leq\int_{t}^{4\varepsilon}|\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))|\,\mathrm{d}s\leq 4\,C

(13.55)

for all
\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in(0,4\varepsilon]
. Combining the bounds (
13.48
), (
13.52
) and (
13.55
) we obtain that there exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
such that

|f_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}-\lambda^{-1}_{\varepsilon\vee t}|\leq\lambda_{\star}^{-1}/2\leq\lambda^{-1}/2\leq\lambda^{-1}_{\varepsilon\vee t}/2

(13.56)

for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
,
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
s\in(0,1]
. This proves the bound (A).

To prove the bound (B) first note that

|\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{1,s}\mathbf{B}_{s}(U(1,0,0))-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))|\\
\leq|\mathbf{c}^{4,0}_{s}(\mathbf{B}_{s}(U(1,0,0))-\mathbf{c}^{4,0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\mathbf{B}_{s}(U(1,0,0))|\\
+|\mathbf{c}^{4,0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\mathbf{B}_{s}(U(1,0,0))-\mathbf{c}^{4,0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))|

(13.57)

where the map
\mathbf{c}^{4,0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
was introduced in Lemma
13.19
. By Lemma
12.6
(A), (C) applied with
\gamma_{1}=\gamma_{2}=-8\kappa
and Lemma
13.13
applied with
\gamma=-8\kappa
there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

\|\mathbf{B}_{s}(U(1,0,0))\|_{\mathscr{W}^{8,3;-16\kappa}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C,\qquad\|\mathbf{B}_{s}(U(1,0,0))-\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{W}}^{2,2;-16\kappa}_{\tau,\varepsilon}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\kappa}

(13.58)

for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
s\in(0,1]
. Consequently, by Lemma
13.19
(B), (C), (D) applied with
m=4
,
i=0
and
\gamma=-16\kappa
there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

|\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{1,s}\mathbf{B}_{s}(U(1,0,0))-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(U(1,0,0))|\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\lambda_{s}^{-8\kappa}\,s^{-1}

(13.59)

for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
s\in(0,1]
. The bound (B) follows now from Lemma
2.4
(E) applied with
\rho=-1-8\kappa
.

Finally, let us turn to the proof of the bounds (C), (D). Observe that

h_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1,0,0)=t\,\mathbf{c}^{2,0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(s\mapsto s^{-1}\,U(1,0,0)),

(13.60)

where the map
\mathbf{c}^{2,0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
was introduced in Lemma
13.19
. The bounds (C), (D) follow now from Lemma
13.19
(A), (D) applied with
m=2
,
i=0
and
\gamma=-8\kappa
as well as Lemma
13.13
(A) applied with
\gamma=-8\kappa
. This finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma 13.26
.

and

0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and

define

(13.61)

where the maps

. Note that it holds

(13.62)

We shall prove that for
i\in\{0,1\}
it holds

(A
1
)

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma-i}\,t^{i}\,|\mathbf{g}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}
,

(B
1
)

.

(C
1
)

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma-i}\,t^{i}\,|\mathbf{g}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{g}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}}
,

(D
1
)

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma-i}\,t^{i}\,|\mathbf{g}^{(i)}_{t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{g}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}

for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in(0,1]
, where
\gamma=-128\kappa
. For
i=1
the above bounds follow from Lemma
13.21
and Def.
13.1
. To prove the above bounds for
i=0
we use the bounds with
i=1
and Lemma
2.4
(E) applied with
\rho=\gamma
. Note that by Lemma
13.25
(A), (B) it holds

\lambda_{t}^{1+10\kappa}\,|f_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}|\leq C\,\lambda_{t}^{10\kappa}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa},\qquad\lambda_{t}^{1+10\kappa}\,|f_{t}-f_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}|\leq C\,\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee\tau}^{\kappa}\,\lambda_{t}^{2\kappa}\leq C\,\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee\tau}^{\kappa}.

(13.63)

The bounds (A), (B), (C),(D) follow from the above bounds and the bounds (A
1
), (B
1
), (C
1
), (D
1
) with
i=0
.

To prove the bound (E) first note that since
\dot{H}_{\varepsilon;s}=0
if
s<\varepsilon
it holds
B_{\varepsilon;s}=0
and
\mathbf{g}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=0
if
s<\varepsilon
. Consequently,
\mathbf{g}^{(0)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=\mathbf{g}^{(0)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;\varepsilon}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})
if
t<\varepsilon
. Recall also that by Lemma
13.25
(A) it holds
f_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\geq\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee t}^{-1}/C
. Consequently, there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that it holds

\mathbf{g}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\geq\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee t}^{-1}/C-C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee t}^{\gamma}=\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee t}^{-1}\,(1/C-C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee t}^{1-128\kappa})\\
\geq\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee t}^{-1}\,(1/C-C\,\lambda^{1-127\kappa})\geq\lambda_{\varepsilon\vee t}^{-1}/(2C)

(13.64)

for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
,
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in(0,1]
provided
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
is small enough. This proves the bound (E) and completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Lemma 13.27
.

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
, all
t\in(0,1]
and all
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
,
Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}
it holds:

(A)

\lambda_{t}^{36\kappa-1}\,t\,|\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}
,

(B)

\lambda_{t}^{36\kappa-1}\,t\,|\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}}
,

(C)

\lambda_{t}^{36\kappa-1}\,t\,|\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}}
,

(D)

\lambda_{t}^{36\kappa-1}\,t\,|\mathbf{r}_{t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}
.

Proof.

We first observe that for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
,
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\equiv(g_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},r_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},W_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\in\mathscr{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and
s\in(0,1]
it holds

\mathbf{r}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=\mathbf{r}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})+\mathbf{r}^{(2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}),

(13.65)

where

\mathbf{r}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=-\int_{t}^{1}\mathbf{r}^{(3)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\,\mathrm{d}s,\qquad\mathbf{r}^{(2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=-h_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}/g_{t}

(13.66)

and

\mathbf{r}^{(3)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}\mathbf{G}_{\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=\mathbf{c}^{2,0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(\mathbf{G}_{\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})).

(13.67)

Recall that the map
\mathbf{G}_{\varepsilon;s}
was introduced in Lemma
13.16
, the maps
\mathbf{a}^{(1)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
,
\mathbf{a}^{(2)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
and
\mathbf{a}^{(3)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
were introduced in Lemma
13.21
, the map
\mathbf{c}^{2,0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
was introduced in Lemma
13.19
and
h_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
was introduced in Def.
13.23
. We shall prove that for
i\in\{1,2,3\}
it holds

(A
1
)

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma(i)}\,t^{-\varrho(i)}\,|\mathbf{r}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}
,

(B
1
)

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma(i)}\,t^{-\varrho(i)}\,|\mathbf{r}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{r}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}}
,

(C
1
)

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma(i)}\,t^{-\varrho(i)}\,|\mathbf{r}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{r}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}}
,

(D
1
)

\lambda_{t}^{-\gamma(i)}\,t^{-\varrho(i)}\,|\mathbf{r}^{(i)}_{t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{r}^{(i)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}
,

where
\gamma(1)=2-76\kappa
,
\varrho(1)=-1
,
\gamma(2)=1-16\kappa
,
\varrho(2)=-1
and
\gamma(3)=1-76\kappa
,
\varrho(3)=-2
. Note that the above bounds with
i\in\{1,2\}
imply immediately the bounds (A), (B), (C), (D). Actually, even stronger bounds with
\lambda_{t}^{36\kappa-1}
replaced by
\lambda_{t}^{16\kappa-1}
hold true.

It remains to prove the bounds (A
1
), (B
1
), (C
1
), (D
1
). Application of Lemma
13.19
with
m=2
,
i=0
and
\gamma=2-80\kappa
and Lemma
13.16
with
(i,j)=(0,0)
yields the bounds (A
1
), (B
1
), (C
1
), (D
1
) with
i=3
. Consequently, the bounds (A
1
), (B
1
), (C
1
), (D
1
) with
i=1
follow from Lemma
2.4
(C) applied with
\varrho=-1
and
\rho=2-76\kappa
. To show the bounds (A
1
), (B
1
), (C
1
), (D
1
) with
i=2
we use Lemma
13.25
(C), (D) and Lemma
13.12
.
∎

Lemma 13.28
.

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
, all
t\in(0,1]
and all
X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
,
Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Y}
it holds:

(A)

\lambda_{t}^{36\kappa-1}\,|\mathbf{z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}
,

(B)

\lambda_{t}^{36\kappa-1}\,|\mathbf{z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Y_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{X}}
,

(C)

\lambda_{t}^{36\kappa-1}\,|\mathbf{z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{X}}}
,

(D)

\lambda_{t}^{36\kappa-1}\,|\mathbf{z}_{t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(Z_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})|\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}
.

Proof.

It holds

\mathbf{z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}):=\int_{0}^{t}(\mathbf{a}^{(4)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})+\mathbf{a}^{(5)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})+\mathbf{a}^{(6)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\,\mathrm{d}s,

(13.68)

where the maps
\mathbf{a}^{(4)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
,
\mathbf{a}^{(5)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
and
\mathbf{a}^{(6)}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
were introduced in Lemma
13.21
. The bounds (A), (B), (C), (D) follow from Lemma
13.21
and Lemma
2.4
(D) applied with
\rho=1-36\kappa
.
∎

14
Relation to Polchinski equation

In this section we construct a solution
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
of the flow equation (
7.13
) using the fixed point
X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
of the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
defined in Sec.
13
. The strategy of the construction was discussed in Sec.
2
. We first construct a solution
V_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
of the flow equation (
14.4
) and subsequently use it to construct a solution
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
of the flow equation (
7.13
).

Theorem 14.1
.

Let
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
and
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, where
\lambda_{\star}
is as in Corollary
13.11
. Let

(0,1]\ni t\mapsto X_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\equiv(g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathscr{N}

(14.1)

be the fixed point of the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\to\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
, introduced in Def.
13.5
. Moreover, set

g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}:=g_{\tau,\varepsilon;\varepsilon},\quad r_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}:=r_{\tau,\varepsilon;\varepsilon},\quad z_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}:=0,\quad W_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}:=0.

(14.2)

For
t\in[0,1]
define

V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\equiv(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}:=U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})+W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{N}.

(14.3)

Then it holds

\langle V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes m}\rangle=\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes m}\rangle+\int_{0}^{t}\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes m}\rangle\,\mathrm{d}s

(14.4)

for all
t\in(0,1]
,
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
.

Remark 14.2
.

Since
\dot{G}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=0
for
t\in(0,\varepsilon]
by Remark
5.2
(A), it follows from Def.
12.4
that
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;t}({\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=0
for all
t\in(0,\varepsilon]
. Consequently, by Def.
13.5
we have

V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}=V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\quad g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}=g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\quad r_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}=r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\quad z_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}=z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\quad W_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}=W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}

(14.5)

for all
t\in(0,\varepsilon]
, where
(g_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},W_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})
is the fixed point of
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
.

Proof.

First note that Eq. (
14.3
) is equivalent to the system of equations

\displaystyle V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}^{m}

\displaystyle=W^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\qquad m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus\{2,4\},

(14.6)

\displaystyle V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}^{4}

\displaystyle=U^{4}\,1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}+W^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},

\displaystyle V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}^{2}

\displaystyle=U^{2}\,r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}+U^{2}_{\partial}\,z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}+W^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},

where
U^{4},U^{2},U^{2}_{\partial}
were introduced in Def.
11.1
. Observe that the fixed point of the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\to\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
, introduced in Def.
13.5
, is equivalent to the system of Eqs. (
14.9
), (
14.10
) and (
14.17
) presented below. These equations imply in particular that

\displaystyle V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}^{m}

\displaystyle=0,\qquad m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus\{2,4\},

(14.7)

\displaystyle V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}^{4}

\displaystyle=U^{4}\,1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},

\displaystyle V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}^{2}

\displaystyle=U^{2}\,r_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}.

As a result, it holds

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}

\displaystyle=0,\qquad m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus\{2,4\},

(14.8)

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}

\displaystyle=U^{4}\,1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}

\displaystyle=U^{2}\,r_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}+1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}\,\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}U(1,0,0).

If
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus\{2,4\}
, then the first term on the RHS of Eq. (
14.4
) vanishes identically and
V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=W^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
. Consequently, for
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus\{2,4\}
Eq. (
14.4
) follows immediately from the fixed point equation

W^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(X_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})=\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s.

(14.9)

Let us turn to the proof of Eq. (
14.4
) for
m=4
. By the fixed point equation we obtain

\displaystyle W^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s-\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{4}W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},

(14.10)

\displaystyle g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}

\displaystyle=\lambda^{-1}+\int_{t}^{1}(g_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})^{2}\,\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s.

In particular, the function
(0,1]\ni t\mapsto g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathbb{R}
is continuously differentiable and

\partial_{t}(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})=-(\partial_{t}g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})/(g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})^{2}=\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;t}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}).

(14.11)

Consequently, we obtain

1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}+\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s.

(14.12)

Next, we observe that by Lemma
11.18
it holds

\langle U^{4}\,\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})+\mathbf{R}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle\\
=\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle.

(14.13)

We also note that it follows from Def.
10.22
of the map
\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s,t}
that
\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{4}W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
depends only on
W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}^{m}
with
m>4
. Hence, taking into account Eq. (
14.9
) we obtain

\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{4}W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s.

(14.14)

Consequently, by Remark
10.25
(C) and the equality
\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}V=\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}V-V
we get

\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{4}W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s-\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s.

(14.15)

Using the above identities we arrive at

\langle V^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle=\langle U^{4}\,1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle+\langle W^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle\\
=\langle U^{4}\,1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle+\int_{0}^{t}\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle\,\mathrm{d}s-\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{4}W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle\\
=\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle+\int_{0}^{t}\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{4}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 4}\rangle\,\mathrm{d}s.

(14.16)

This proves Eq. (
14.4
) for
m=4
. It remains to show that Eq. (
14.4
) holds true for
m=2
. Observe that by the fixed point equation we obtain

\displaystyle W^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s-\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},

(14.17)

\displaystyle r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=

\displaystyle-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},0,0)-\int_{t}^{1}\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s,

\displaystyle z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{L}_{\partial}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s.

The second of the above equations implies

r_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}=-\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,0}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},0,0)-\int_{0}^{1}\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s,

(14.18)

and

r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=r_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}+\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},0,0)+\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}-1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},0,0)\\
+\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\,\mathrm{d}s,

(14.19)

where we used the identity

\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,0}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},0,0)=\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},0,0)+\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},0,0).

(14.20)

Using Def.
10.22
of the maps
\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s,t}
and
\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s,t}
and Remark
10.25
(C) we show that

\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}-\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})\\
=\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}-\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},0,0)

(14.21)

and

\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}=\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},0,0)\\
=(\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,0}-\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0})U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},0,0)=\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},0,0).

(14.22)

Moreover, we observe that
\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
depends only on
V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}^{m}
with
m>2
. Hence, by Eq. (
14.4
) with
m>2
, which has already been proved to be true, and Eq. (
14.22
) we obtain

\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle=\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},0,0),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\\
+\int_{0}^{t}\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\,\mathrm{d}s.

(14.23)

Consequently, by Remark
10.25
(C) and Eq. (
14.21
) it holds

\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle=\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}-1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},0,0),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\\
+\int_{0}^{t}\langle(\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}-\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s})\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\,\mathrm{d}s.

(14.24)

Using Lemma
11.18
and the fact that
\mathbf{R}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}-1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},0,0)=0
as well as
\mathbf{L}_{\partial}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}-1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},0,0)=0
we obtain

\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle=\langle U^{2}\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}-1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},0,0),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\\
+\int_{0}^{t}\langle(\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}-\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s})\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\,\mathrm{d}s.

(14.25)

Next, we observe that by Lemma
11.18
it holds

\langle U^{2}\,\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})+U^{2}_{\partial}\,\mathbf{L}_{\partial}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})+\mathbf{R}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\\
=\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle.

(14.26)

Hence, by the first and third of Eqs. (
14.17
) and Eq. (
14.19
) we obtain

\langle V^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle=\langle U^{2}\,r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}+U^{2}_{\partial}\,z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}+W^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\\[5.0pt]
=\langle U^{2}\,r_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}+U^{2}\,\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},0,0)+U^{2}\,\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}-1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},0,0),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\\[2.0pt]
+\int_{0}^{t}\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\,\mathrm{d}s-\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,t}^{2}W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle.

(14.27)

Consequently, by Eq. (
14.25
) we have

\langle V^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle=\langle U^{2}\,r_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}+U^{2}\,\mathbf{L}\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},0,0),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\\
+\int_{0}^{t}\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),(\mathbf{J}\varphi)^{\otimes 2}\rangle\,\mathrm{d}s.

(14.28)

Hence, Eq. (
14.4
) is true for
m=2
. This finishes the proof.
∎

Corollary 14.3
.

Let
