\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
and
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, where
\lambda_{\star}
is as in Corollary
13.11
. For
t\in[0,1]
define
V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\in\mathscr{N}
as in the statement of Theorem
14.1
. For
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
t\in[0,1]
define the antisymmetric distribution
U^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2m})^{\mathbb{G}^{m}}
by the equality

\langle U^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\phi^{\otimes m}\rangle_{\tau}=\langle V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\mathbf{J}(\chi_{\tau}\phi)\otimes(\mathbf{J}\phi)^{\otimes(m-1)}\rangle

(14.29)

for all
\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
. For
t\in[0,1]
define the functional
\tilde{U}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{N}(C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}})
by the equality

\tilde{U}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(\phi):=\sum_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\langle U^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\phi^{\otimes m}\rangle_{\tau}\in\mathscr{G},\qquad\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}.

(14.30)

For
t\in[0,1]
define
U^{0}\in\mathbb{C}
by the equality

U^{0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=\mathbf{E}U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0})+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{E}\langle\mathrm{D}_{\phi}\tilde{U}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}),\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}\ast\mathrm{D}_{\phi}\tilde{U}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s})\rangle_{\tau}\,\mathrm{d}s.

(14.31)

For
t\in[0,1]
define the functional
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{N}(C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}})
by the equality

U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(\phi):=\sum_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}\langle U^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\phi^{\otimes m}\rangle_{\tau}\in\mathscr{G},\qquad\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}.

(14.32)

Finally, define the functional
U_{\tau,\varepsilon}\in\mathscr{N}(C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}})

as specified in Def.
4.16
with
g_{\tau,\varepsilon}:=g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}
and
r_{\tau,\varepsilon}:=r_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}
, where
g_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}
and
r_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}
are as in the statement of Theorem
14.1
.
Then the map

[0,1]\ni t\mapsto U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{N}(C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}})

is continuous and is a solution of the integral form of the Polchinski equation

U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(\phi)=\mathbf{E}U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}+\phi)\\
+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{E}\langle\mathrm{D}_{\phi}U_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}+\phi),\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}\ast\mathrm{D}_{\phi}U_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}+\phi)\rangle_{\tau}\,\mathrm{d}s

(14.33)

for all
\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
.

Proof.

Using Theorem
14.1
and the fact that the distributions
V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
,
\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0}
and
\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})
are antisymmetric we obtain

\langle V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\mathbf{J}^{\otimes m}\psi\rangle=\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;0},\mathbf{J}^{\otimes m}\psi\rangle+\int_{0}^{t}\langle\mathbf{E}\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}),\mathbf{J}^{\otimes m}\psi\rangle\,\mathrm{d}s

(14.34)

for all
t\in(0,1]
,
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
\psi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{G}^{m}}
. Hence, by Def.
10.22
and
12.4
of the maps
\mathbf{A}^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}
and
\mathbf{B}_{\varepsilon;s}
we have

U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(\phi)=\mathbf{E}U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}+\phi)\\
+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{E}\langle\mathrm{D}_{\phi}U_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}+\phi),\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}\ast\mathrm{D}_{\phi}U_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,s}+\phi)\rangle_{\tau}\,\mathrm{d}s

(14.35)

for all
t\in(0,1)
and
\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
. Finally, to prove the continuity of the map
[0,1]\ni t\mapsto U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{N}(C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}})
we observe that the map is constant for on
[0,\varepsilon]
and use its definition in terms of the fixed point of the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\to\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
as well as Def.
13.1
of the set
\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and Def.
10.32
of the space
\mathscr{W}_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{\alpha,\beta;\gamma}
. This finishes the proof.
∎

Remark 14.4
.

In the presence of the UV and IR cutoffs
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
it is straightforward to show that
U^{0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathbb{C}
given by Eq. (
14.31
) is well-defined for all
t\in[0,1]
. Consequently, for the choice of parameters
g_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and
r_{\tau,\varepsilon}
as in the above corollary it holds

\mathbf{E}\exp(U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t,0}))=\exp(U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}(0))=\exp(U^{0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})\neq 0.

(14.36)

In particular,

\mathbf{E}\exp(U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon}))=\exp(U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t=1}(0))=\exp(U^{0}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t=1})\neq 0.

(14.37)

15
Convergence of Schwinger functions

In this section we establish a relation between an effective potential
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
at the scale
t=1
and the generating functional of the Schwinger functions and complete the proof of Theorem
1.1
. Recall that the effective potential
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
was constructed in Sec.
14
with the use of the fixed point
X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
of the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
constructed in Sec.
13
.

Lemma 15.1
.

Let
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
. Suppose that
\mathbf{E}\exp(U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon}))\neq 0
. The following equality

\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\exp(\langle{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\phi\rangle_{\tau}))=\exp(\langle\phi,G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi\rangle_{\tau}/2+U_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi)-U_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}(0))

(15.1)

holds for all
\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2}_{\tau})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
, where the interacting measure
\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}
was introduced in Def.
4.16
and
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}
is an effective potential at unit scale introduced in Def.
7.2
.

Proof.

First observe that by Def.
4.16
of the measure
\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}
it holds

\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\exp(\langle{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\phi\rangle_{\tau}))=\frac{\int\exp(-A_{\tau}(\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})+U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})+\langle\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon},\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\phi\rangle_{\tau})\,\mathrm{d}\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}}{\int\exp(-A_{\tau}(\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})+U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}))\,\mathrm{d}\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}}

(15.2)

for all
\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2}_{\tau})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
.
Using Def.
4.16
of the free action
A_{\tau}
one proves the following identity

\langle\psi,\phi\rangle_{\tau}-A_{\tau}(\psi)=\frac{1}{2}\langle G\ast\phi,\phi\rangle_{\tau}-A_{\tau}(\psi-G\ast\phi)

(15.3)

for all
\psi,\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2}_{\tau})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
. Consequently, using the equalities

\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast G\ast\vartheta_{\varepsilon}=G_{\varepsilon},\qquad G_{\varepsilon}\ast\phi=G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi

(15.4)

we obtain

\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\exp(\langle{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\phi\rangle_{\tau}))\\
=\exp(\langle\phi,G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi\rangle_{\tau}/2)\,\frac{\int\exp(-A_{\tau}(\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}-G\ast\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\phi)+U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}))\,\mathrm{d}\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}}{\int\exp(-A_{\tau}(\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})+U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}))\,\mathrm{d}\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}}

(15.5)

for all
\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2}_{\tau})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
. Note that since
\mathbf{F}_{\tau}\mathbf{P}_{\tau}\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\subset\Lambda_{\tau,\varepsilon}
it holds

G\ast\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\phi(x)=\tau^{2}\sum_{p\in\Lambda_{\tau,\varepsilon}}(\mathbf{F}_{\tau}G\ast\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\phi)(p)\,\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}p\cdot x}\,\mathrm{d}p

(15.6)

and
G\ast\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\phi\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
for all
\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2}_{\tau})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
, where
\mathcal{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
was introduced in Def.
4.5
. Taking advantage of the invariance under translation of the Berezin integral, which was stated in Lemma
4.12
, we show the following equality

\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\exp(\langle{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\phi\rangle_{\tau}))\\
=\exp(\langle\phi,G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi\rangle_{\tau}/2)\,\frac{\int\exp(-A_{\tau}(\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})+U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast(\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}+G\ast\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\phi)))\,\mathrm{d}\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}}{\int\exp(-A_{\tau}(\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})+U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}))\,\mathrm{d}\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}}.

(15.7)

The above equality can be rewritten as

\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\exp(\langle{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\phi\rangle_{\tau}))\\
=\exp(\langle\phi,G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi\rangle_{\tau}/2)\,\frac{\int\exp(-A_{\tau}(\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})+U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}+G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi))\,\mathrm{d}\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}}{\int\exp(-A_{\tau}(\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})+U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}))\,\mathrm{d}\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}}

(15.8)

for all
\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2}_{\tau})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
. Using Lemma
6.15
we arrive at

\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\exp(\langle{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\phi\rangle_{\tau}))=\exp(\langle\phi,G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi\rangle_{\tau}/2)\,\frac{\mathbf{E}(\exp(U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon}+G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi)))}{\mathbf{E}(\exp(U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon})))}.

(15.9)

The statement follows now from the fact that
G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi\in\mathcal{C}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
for all
\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2}_{\tau})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
,
\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon}=\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;1,0}
and Def.
7.2
of an effective potential.
∎

Definition 15.2
.

Let
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
. Suppose that
\mathbf{E}\exp(U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon}))\neq 0
. We call

\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}\ni\varphi\mapsto Z_{\tau,\varepsilon}[\varphi]:=\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\exp(\langle{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\varphi\rangle))\in\mathscr{G}

(15.10)

the generating functional. The Schwinger function of order
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
is defined by the equation

S^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon}:=\mathrm{D}_{\varphi}^{m}Z_{\tau,\varepsilon}[\varphi]\big|_{\varphi=0}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{G}^{m}}.

(15.11)

The truncated Schwinger function of order
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
is defined by the equation

T^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon}:=\mathrm{D}_{\varphi}^{m}\log(Z_{\tau,\varepsilon}[\varphi])\big|_{\varphi=0}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{G}^{m}}.

(15.12)

Remark 15.3
.

The Schwinger function can be expressed in terms of the truncated Schwinger functions using the formula

\langle S^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon},\varphi^{\otimes m}\rangle=\sum_{\pi\in\Pi_{m}}\prod_{S\in\pi}\langle T^{|S|}_{\tau,\varepsilon},\varphi^{\otimes|S|}\rangle

(15.13)

valid for all
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
, where
\Pi_{m}
denotes the set of partitions of the set
\{1,\ldots,m\}
into disjoint subsets whose union coincides with
\{1,\ldots,m\}
and
|S|
denotes the number of elements of a set
S
.

Theorem 15.4
.

Fix
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, where
\lambda_{\star}
is as in Corollary
13.11
. For all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
let

(0,1]\ni t\mapsto X_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\equiv(g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathscr{N}

(15.14)

be the fixed point of the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\to\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
, which was introduced in Def.
13.5
. Moreover, set

g_{\tau,\varepsilon}:=g_{\tau,\varepsilon;\varepsilon},\quad r_{\tau,\varepsilon}:=r_{\tau,\varepsilon;\varepsilon},

(15.15)

Suppose that for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
the interacting measure
\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}
is defined as specified in Def.
4.16
with the above choice of the parameters
g_{\tau,\varepsilon}
and
r_{\tau,\varepsilon}
. Then for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
there exist
S^{m},T^{m}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{G}^{m}}
such that:

(A)

\lim_{\tau,\varepsilon\searrow 0}\langle T^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon},\varphi\rangle=\langle T^{m},\varphi\rangle
for all
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{G}^{m}}
,

(B)

\lim_{\tau,\varepsilon\searrow 0}\langle S^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon},\varphi\rangle=\langle S^{m},\varphi\rangle
for for all
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{G}^{m}}
,

(C)

S^{m},T^{m}
are invariant under the symmetries of the plane in the sense of Def
8.2
,

(D)

T^{m}
has the properties stated in Items (C) and (D) of Theorem
1.1
.

(E)

\lim_{|x|\to\infty}\exp(|x|^{1/2})\,|\langle T^{m},\varphi_{x}\otimes\psi\rangle|=0
for all
n\in\{1,\ldots,m-1\}
,
\varphi\in C^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}(\mathbb{R}^{2n})^{\mathbb{G}^{n}}
,
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}(\mathbb{R}^{2(m-n)})^{\mathbb{G}^{m-n}}
, where for
x\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
we define
\varphi_{x}\in C^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}(\mathbb{R}^{2n})^{\mathbb{G}^{n}}
by the equality
\varphi_{x}(y_{1},\ldots,y_{n}):=\varphi(y_{1}-x,\ldots,y_{n}-x)
for all
y_{1},\ldots,y_{n}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
.

Remark 15.5
.

Note that
g_{\tau,\varepsilon}=g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
,
r_{\tau,\varepsilon}=r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
for all
t\in(0,\varepsilon]
by Remark
14.2
.

Remark 15.6
.

The exponent
1/2
in Item (C) of the above theorem has no significance. With some extra work, it is possible to prove exponential decay of the truncated correlations. To this end, one has to use a decomposition
(G_{\varepsilon,t})_{t\in[0,1]}
of the covariance
G_{\varepsilon}
at
\varepsilon=0
, which in contrast to the decomposition introduced in Sec.
5
, has exponential decay.

Proof.

First, note that by Corollary
13.11
for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\to\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
is well-defined and has a unique fixed point
X_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\equiv(g_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}},W_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}})\in\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
. For all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in(0,1]
we define
V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=(V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\in\mathscr{N}
by the equation

V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}[\varphi]:=U(1/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})[\varphi]+W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}[\varphi]

(15.16)

for all
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
. Recall that we omit
\tau
if
\tau=0
and we omit
\varepsilon
if
\varepsilon=0
. Moreover, for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
and
t\in[0,1]
we define
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{N}(C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}})
as in Corollary
14.3
. Then by Corollary
14.3
and Lemma
7.7
for all
t\in[0,1]
the functional
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{N}(C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}})
is an effective potential at scale
t
. Since by Remark
14.4
we have
\mathbf{E}\exp(U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon}))\neq 0
using Lemma
15.1
we conclude that

\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\exp(\langle{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\phi\rangle_{\tau}))=\exp(\langle\phi,G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi\rangle_{\tau}/2+U_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast_{\tau}\phi)-U_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}(0))

(15.17)

for all
\phi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2}_{\tau})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
and
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
.

Let us prove Items (A) and (B). We observe that it suffices to show that for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
there exist
S^{m},T^{m}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{G}^{m}}
such that:

(A’)

\lim_{\tau,\varepsilon\searrow 0}\langle T^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon},\varphi_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{m}\rangle=\langle T^{m},\varphi_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{m}\rangle
for all
\varphi_{1},\ldots,\varphi_{m}\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}
,

(B’)

\lim_{\tau,\varepsilon\searrow 0}\langle S^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon},\varphi_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{m}\rangle=\langle S^{m},\varphi_{1}\otimes\ldots\otimes\varphi_{m}\rangle
for all
\varphi_{1},\ldots,\varphi_{m}\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}
.

By Remark
15.3
Item (B’) follows from Item (A’). We proceed to the proof of Item (A’). For
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
we apply Eq. (
15.17
) with
\phi=\mathbf{P}_{\tau}\varphi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
to obtain

\log(Z_{\tau,\varepsilon}[\varphi])=\log(\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\exp(\langle{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\varphi\rangle)))=\langle\varphi,G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi\rangle/2+U_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi)-U_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}(0))

(15.18)

for
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
. Note that by the translational invariance of
V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
Eq. (
14.29
) implies that

\langle U^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi)^{\otimes m}\rangle_{\tau}=\langle V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\mathbf{J}(G_{\varepsilon}\ast\varphi)\otimes(\mathbf{J}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi))^{\otimes(m-1)}\rangle.

(15.19)

for all
m\in\mathbb{N}+
,
\tau,\varepsilon,t\in(0,1]
and
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
.
Consequently, using Def.
15.2
we arrive at

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{2},\varphi^{\otimes 2}\rangle

\displaystyle:=\langle\varphi,G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi\rangle+2\,\langle V^{2}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1},\mathbf{J}(G_{\varepsilon}\ast\varphi)\otimes(\mathbf{J}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi))\rangle,

(15.20)

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau,\varepsilon}^{m},\varphi^{\otimes m}\rangle

\displaystyle:=m!\,\langle V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1},\mathbf{J}(G_{\varepsilon}\ast\varphi)\otimes(\mathbf{J}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi))^{\otimes(m-1)}\rangle,\qquad m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus\{2\},

for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
and
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
. For
m\in\mathbb{N}+
we define an antisymmetric distribution
T^{m}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{G}^{m}}
by the equalities

\displaystyle\langle T^{2},\varphi^{\otimes 2}\rangle

\displaystyle:=\langle\varphi,G\ast\varphi\rangle+2\,\langle V^{2}_{1},(\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi))^{\otimes 2}\rangle,

(15.21)

\displaystyle\langle T^{m},\varphi^{\otimes m}\rangle

\displaystyle:=m!\,\langle V^{m}_{1},(\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi))^{\otimes m}\rangle,\qquad m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}\setminus\{2\},

for all
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}^{-}
, where
V_{1}=(V_{1}^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\in\mathscr{N}
is defined by Eq. (
15.16
) with
\tau=0
,
\varepsilon=0
and
t=1
. Since by Lemma
15.10

\lim_{\tau,\varepsilon\searrow 0}\langle\varphi_{1},G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi_{2}\rangle=\langle\varphi_{1},G\ast\varphi_{2}\rangle

(15.22)

for all
\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2}\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}
in order to prove Item (A’) it suffices to show that

\lim_{\tau,\varepsilon\searrow 0}\langle V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1},\mathbf{J}(G_{\varepsilon}\ast\varphi_{1})\otimes\mathbf{J}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi_{2})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathbf{J}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi_{m})\rangle\\
=\langle V^{m}_{1},\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi_{1})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi_{m})\rangle

(15.23)

for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
\varphi_{1},\ldots,\varphi_{m}\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}
. To this end, we observe that

|\langle V^{m}_{1},\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi_{1})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi_{m})\rangle-\langle V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1},\mathbf{J}(G_{\varepsilon}\ast\varphi_{1})\otimes\mathbf{J}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi_{2})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathbf{J}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi_{m})\rangle|\\
\leq|\langle V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1},\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi_{1})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi_{m})-\mathbf{J}(G_{\varepsilon}\ast\varphi_{1})\otimes\mathbf{J}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi_{2})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathbf{J}(G_{\tau,\varepsilon}\ast\varphi_{m})\rangle|\\
+|\langle V^{m}_{1}-V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1},\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi_{1})\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi_{m})\rangle|

(15.24)

By Remark
15.8
the above expression is bounded by

\|V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}\,(A+B_{\tau,\varepsilon})^{m-1}\,B_{\tau,\varepsilon}+\|V^{m}_{1}-V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}\,A^{m},

(15.25)

where

A:=\|\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi_{1})/\tilde{w}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}}\vee\|\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}}\vee\ldots\vee\|\mathbf{J}(G\ast\varphi_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}}

(15.26)

and

B_{\tau,\varepsilon}:=\|\mathbf{J}((G-G_{\varepsilon})\ast\varphi_{1})/\tilde{w}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}}\\
+\|\mathbf{J}((G-G_{\tau,\varepsilon})\ast\varphi_{2})\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}}+\ldots+\|\mathbf{J}((G-G_{\tau,\varepsilon})\ast\varphi_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}}.

(15.27)

We used above the notation introduced in Remark
15.7
below. Note that by the bounds (
13.10
) established in Corollary
13.11
and Def.
10.12
and
10.15
there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

\|V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}\|_{\mathscr{N}^{m}}\leq C\,,\qquad\|V^{m}_{1}-V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}

(15.28)

for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
and
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
. Item (A’) follows now from Lemma
15.10
and the bounds for
\|V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}\leq\|V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}\|_{\mathscr{N}^{m}}
and
\|V^{m}_{1}-V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;1}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}
stated above. As argued above, this proves Item (B’) as well as Items (A) and (B).

Let us turn to the proof of Item (C). Note that
V_{1}\in\mathscr{N}
defined by Eq. (
15.16
) is invariant under the symmetries of the plane by Def.
11.1
,
13.1
and
10.32
. This together with the fact that the kernel
G
is invariant under the symmetries of the plane implies Item (C).

Item (D) is an immediate consequence of the definition of

T^{m}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{G}^{m}}
given above, the properties of
V_{1}=(V_{1}^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}\in\mathscr{N}
, the fact that
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}G(x)\,\mathrm{d}x=1
and Def.
13.5
.

In order to prove Item (E) it is enough to show that

\lim_{x\to\infty}\exp(|x|^{1/2})\,|\langle\varphi_{x},G\ast\psi\rangle|=0

(15.29)

for all
\varphi\in C^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}
and
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}
and

\lim_{x\to\infty}\exp(|x|^{1/2})\,|\langle V^{m}_{1},\mathbf{J}^{\otimes m}(G^{\otimes m}\ast(\varphi_{x}\otimes\psi))\rangle|=0

(15.30)

for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
,
n\in\{1,\ldots,m-1\}
,
\varphi\in C^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}(\mathbb{R}^{2n})^{\mathbb{G}^{n}}
,
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}(\mathbb{R}^{2(m-n)})^{\mathbb{G}^{m-n}}
. Both of the above equalities follow from Remarks
15.8
and
15.9
and the bounds (
15.28
) for
\|V^{m}_{1}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}\leq\|V^{m}_{1}\|_{\mathscr{N}^{m}}
. This finishes the proof.
∎

Remark 15.7
.

Given
p\in[1,\infty]
and a finite tuple
\varphi=(\varphi^{k})_{k\in\mathbb{K}}
of measurable functions over
\mathbb{R}^{2}
we define
\|\varphi\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{K}}}:=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{K}}\|\varphi^{k}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{2})}
.

Remark 15.8
.

Let
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
V=(V^{a,\sigma})_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\in\mathscr{N}^{m}
. Recall that

\|V\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}=\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|\tilde{w}_{1}^{m}V^{a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\leq\|V\|_{\mathscr{N}^{m}},

(15.31)

where

\tilde{w}^{m}_{1}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m})=\tilde{w}(x_{1})\exp(\mathrm{D}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m})^{\zeta}),\qquad\tilde{w}(x)=(1+|x|)^{-1/2},\qquad\zeta=4/5,

(15.32)

for all
x,x_{1},\ldots,x_{m}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
and
\mathrm{D}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m})\geq 0
is the diameter of
\{x_{1},\ldots,x_{m}\}\subset\mathbb{R}^{2}
. The following bound

|\langle V,\varphi\rangle|\leq\|V\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\sup_{x_{2},\ldots,x_{m}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{|\varphi^{a,\sigma}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m})|}{\tilde{w}_{1}^{m}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m})}\,\mathrm{d}x_{1}

(15.33)

is true for all
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2m})^{\mathbb{A}^{m}\times\mathbb{G}^{m}}
.

Remark 15.9
.

By Lemma
5.3
and Remark
5.2
(E), (F) there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

\|x\mapsto(1+|x|)^{1/2}\,\exp(|x|^{\zeta})\,G(x)\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}^{2}}}\leq C,\qquad\zeta=4/5.

(15.34)

Consequently, by Lemma
9.5
(c) the following bound

\|G^{\otimes m}\ast V\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}\leq C^{m}\,\|V\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{N}}^{m}}

(15.35)

holds for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
and
V\in\mathscr{N}^{m}
.

Lemma 15.10
.

The following equalities

\lim_{\varepsilon\searrow 0}\|((G-G_{\varepsilon})\ast\varphi)/\tilde{w}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}}=0,\qquad\lim_{\tau,\varepsilon\searrow 0}\|(G-G_{\tau,\varepsilon})\ast\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}}=0

(15.36)

hold for all
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}
.

Proof.

By Remark
9.4
we have

\|((G-G_{\varepsilon})\ast\varphi)/\tilde{w}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}}\leq\|(G-G_{\varepsilon})/\tilde{w}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}^{2}}}\,\|\varphi/\tilde{w}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}}

(15.37)

Moreover, by elementary estimates we obtain

\|(G-G_{\tau,\varepsilon})\ast\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}}\leq\|(G-G_{\varepsilon})\ast\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}}+\|(G_{\varepsilon}-G_{\tau,\varepsilon})\ast\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}}\\
\leq\|(G-G_{\varepsilon})\ast\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}}+\|G_{\varepsilon}\ast(\varphi-\mathbf{P}_{\tau}\varphi)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}}\\
\leq\|G-G_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}^{2}}}\,\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}}+\|G_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}^{2}}}\,\|\varphi-\mathbf{P}_{\tau}\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}}

(15.38)

Note that by Lemma
5.3
and Remark
5.2
(E) there exists
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

\|\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;t}/\tilde{w}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}^{2}}}\leq C

(15.39)

for all
\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in(0,1]
. Consequently, by Remark
5.2
(F) we obtain

\|G_{\varepsilon}/\tilde{w}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}^{2}}}\leq C,\qquad\|G-G_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}^{2}}}\leq\|(G-G_{\varepsilon})/\tilde{w}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}^{2}}}\leq 4\,\varepsilon\,C

(15.40)

for all
\varepsilon\in[0,1]
. This finishes the proof.
∎

16
Interacting Gross-Neveu field

In this section we construct the interacting Gross-Neveu field in the non-commutative probability space
(\mathscr{F},\mathbf{E})
introduced in Sec.
6
. To this end, we study the following stochastic differential equation

\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=-\int_{t}^{1}\dot{G}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}\ast_{\tau}\mathrm{D}U_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}[\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}]\,\mathrm{d}s+\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}

(16.1)

for
(0,1]\ni t\mapsto\,\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{C}^{\mathbb{G}}
. The functional
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}[\varphi]
is the effective potential,
\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
is the scale decomposition of the free field,
\ast_{\tau}
denotes the convolution on the torus
\mathbb{T}_{\tau}
and
G_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
is the periodization of the scale decomposition of the propagator
G_{\varepsilon;t}
. For the motivation behind the above equation see Remark 2.2 in
[
BB21
]
and Sec. 4 of
[
BBD23
]
. Let us only note that the solution of the above equation satisfies for all
F\in\mathscr{N}(C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}})
the following identity

\mathbf{E}F(\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})=\frac{\mathbf{E}F(\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})\exp(U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}[\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}])}{\mathbf{E}\exp(U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}[\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}])},

(16.2)

which was proved in Prop. 3.10 of
[
DFG22
]
. In particular, by Lemma
7.1
and
U_{\tau,\varepsilon}=U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t=0}
the field
\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon}=\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t=0}
is distributed according to the Gross-Neveu measure
\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}
with cutoffs
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
. We call
\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon}
the interacting Gross-Neveu field. We study its properties and complete the proof of Theorem
1.5
.

Definition 16.1
.

The vector space
\mathscr{Z}
consists of functions
\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in C((0,1],\mathscr{C}^{\mathbb{G}})
such that the following norm

\|\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{Z}}:=\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\sup_{t\in(0,1]}\lambda^{\kappa}\,t^{1/2+|a|}\,\|\partial^{a}\varPhi^{\sigma}_{t}\|_{\mathscr{C}}

(16.3)

is finite. For
\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Z}
we define

\|\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{Z}}}:=\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\sup_{t\in(0,1]}\lambda^{\kappa}\,\lambda_{t}^{\kappa}\,t^{1/2+|a|}\,\|\tilde{w}\,\partial^{a}\varPhi^{\sigma}_{t}\|_{\mathscr{C}},

(16.4)

where
\tilde{w}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
is defined by the equality
\tilde{w}(x)=(1+|x|)^{-1/2}
for all
x\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
. We also define

\mathscr{U}:=\{\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Z}\,|\,\|\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{Z}}\leq 1\}.

(16.5)

Remark 16.2
.

Recall that the vector space
(\mathscr{C},\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{C}})
was introduced in Def.
6.18
. It is easy to see that
(\mathscr{C},\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{C}})
and
(\mathscr{Z},\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{Z}})
are Banach spaces.

Definition 16.3
.

Let
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, where
\lambda_{\star}
is as in Corollary
13.11
. Let

(0,1]\ni t\mapsto X_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\equiv(g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathscr{N}

(16.6)

be the fixed point of the map
\mathbf{X}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\to\mathscr{Y}_{\tau,\varepsilon}
, introduced in Def.
13.5
. For
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and
t\in(0,1]
define

V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\equiv(V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}:=U(\theta^{2}_{\varepsilon;t}/g_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},r_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},z_{\tau,\varepsilon;t})+W_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{N}

(16.7)

and set
V_{t}:=V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
with
\tau=0
,
\varepsilon=0
. For
k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
,
\varphi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}
we define

\mathrm{D}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}[\varphi]\equiv(\mathrm{D}^{1,a,\sigma}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}[\varphi])_{a\in\mathbb{A},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\in L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}\otimes_{\mathrm{alg}}\mathscr{G}

(16.8)

by the equality

\langle\mathrm{D}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}[\varphi],\psi\rangle\equiv\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A},\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\langle\mathrm{D}^{1,a,\sigma}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}[\varphi],\psi^{a,\sigma}\rangle:=\sum_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}m\,\langle V^{m}_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\varphi^{\otimes(m-1)}\otimes\psi\rangle\in\mathscr{G}

(16.9)

for all
\psi\in\mathscr{S}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{A}\times\mathbb{G}}
.

Remark 16.4
.

Recall that
\theta_{\varepsilon;t}
was introduced in Def.
13.2
and
\theta_{\varepsilon;t}=1
unless
\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;t}=0
.

Definition 16.5
.

For
\lambda\in(0,1]
small enough and
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
we define a map

\mathbf{Z}_{\varepsilon,\tau;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{U}\to\mathscr{Z}

by the equality

\mathbf{Z}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}[\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}]:=\int_{t}^{1}\mathbf{J}\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}\ast\mathrm{D}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}[\mathbf{J}\varPhi_{s}]\,\mathrm{d}s+\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},

(16.10)

where

(\mathbf{J}\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;t}\ast\mathrm{D}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}[\mathbf{J}\varPhi_{t}])^{\sigma}:=\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}}(-1)^{|a|}\,\partial^{a}\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;t}\ast\mathrm{D}^{1,a,\sigma}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}[\mathbf{J}\varPhi_{t}].

(16.11)

We omit
\tau
and
\varepsilon
if
\tau=0
and
\varepsilon=0
.

Remark 16.6
.

The fact that the map
\mathbf{Z}_{\varepsilon,\tau;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
is well-defined is non-trivial and is a consequence of the estimates stated below.

Theorem 16.7
.

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
, all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
and all
\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}},\varPsi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{U}
it holds:

(A)

\|s\mapsto\mathbf{Z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})\|_{\mathscr{Z}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}
,

(B)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{Z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{Z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPsi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\mathscr{Z}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-\varPsi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{Z}}
,

(C)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{Z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{Z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPsi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{Z}}}\leq C\,\lambda^{\kappa}\,\|\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}-\varPsi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{Z}}}
,

(D)

\|s\mapsto(\mathbf{Z}_{s}(\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\mathbf{Z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}))\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{Z}}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}
,

(E)

\|s\mapsto\lambda_{s}^{40\kappa-1}(\mathbf{Z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}(\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;s})\|_{\mathscr{Z}}\leq C
.

Proof.

We write
\mathbf{Z}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}[\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}]=\mathbf{Z}^{(1)}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}[\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}]+\mathbf{Z}^{(2)}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}[\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}]
, where

\mathbf{Z}^{(1)}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}[\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}]:=\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t},\qquad\mathbf{Z}^{(2)}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}[\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}]:=\int_{t}^{1}\mathbf{J}\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}\ast\mathrm{D}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}[\mathbf{J}\varPhi_{s}]\,\mathrm{d}s,

(16.12)

and prove the bounds (A), (B), (C), (D) separately for
\mathbf{Z}^{(1)}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}
and
\mathbf{Z}^{(2)}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}
. The bound (E) involves only
\mathbf{Z}^{(2)}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}
. The bounds (A), (B), (C), (D) for
\mathbf{Z}^{(1)}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}
follow immediately from Lemma
6.20
. Thus, it remains to prove the bounds for
\mathbf{Z}^{(2)}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}
.

First note that the weights introduced in Def.
9.1
satisfy the estimate

\tilde{w}(x_{1})\leq\tilde{w}^{m}_{s}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m})\leq w^{m}_{s}(x_{1},\ldots,x_{m})\tilde{w}(x_{m})

(16.13)

for all
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
,
x_{1},\ldots,x_{m}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
and
s\in[0,1]
. Using Lemma
12.3
, Def.
6.18
of
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{C}}
and
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{C}}}
and the above estimate with
m=2
we obtain

\begin{gathered}\|\partial^{a}\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}\ast\varphi\|_{\mathscr{C}}\leq C\,s^{-|a|}\,\|\varphi\|_{\mathscr{C}},\qquad\|\partial^{a}\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;s}\ast\varphi\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{C}}}\leq C\,s^{-|a|}\,\|\varphi\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{C}}},\\
\|\partial^{a}(\dot{G}^{\sigma}_{s}-\dot{G}^{\sigma}_{\varepsilon;s})\ast\varphi\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{C}}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,\lambda_{s}^{-\kappa}\,s^{-|a|}\,\|\varphi\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{C}}}.\end{gathered}

(16.14)

By Def.
16.3
of
\mathrm{D}^{1,a,\sigma}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}
and Def.
10.6
of
\|{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{M}}
we have

\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\mathrm{D}^{1,a,\sigma}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}[\mathbf{J}\varPhi_{s}]\|_{\mathscr{C}}\leq\sum_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}m\left(\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}^{m,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\right)\left(\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\partial^{a}\varPhi_{s}^{\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{C}}\right)^{m-1}

(16.15)

and

\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\mathrm{D}^{1,a,\sigma}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}[\mathbf{J}\varPhi_{s}]-\mathrm{D}^{1,a,\sigma}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}[\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{s}]\|_{\mathscr{C}}\leq\sum_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}m^{2}\left(\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}^{m,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\right)\\
\times\left(\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\partial^{a}\varPhi_{s}^{\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{C}}+\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\partial^{a}\varPsi_{s}^{\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{C}}\right)^{m-1}\,\left(\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\partial^{a}(\varPhi^{\sigma}_{s}-\varPsi^{\sigma}_{s})\|_{\mathscr{C}}\right)

(16.16)

Similarly, using the estimate (
16.13
) we show that

\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\mathrm{D}^{1,a,\sigma}V_{s}[\mathbf{J}\varPhi_{s}]-\mathrm{D}^{1,a,\sigma}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}[\mathbf{J}\varPhi_{s}]\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{C}}}\\
\leq\sum_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}m\left(\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|\tilde{w}^{m}_{s}(V_{s}^{m,a,\sigma}-V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}^{m,a,\sigma})\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\right)\left(\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\partial^{a}\varPhi_{s}^{\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{C}}\right)^{m-1}

(16.17)

and

\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\mathrm{D}^{1,a,\sigma}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}[\mathbf{J}\varPhi_{s}]-\mathrm{D}^{1,a,\sigma}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}[\mathbf{J}\varPsi_{s}]\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{C}}}\leq\sum_{m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}}m^{2}\left(\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|w_{s}^{m}V_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}^{m,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\right)\\
\times\left(\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\partial^{a}\varPhi_{s}^{\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{C}}+\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\partial^{a}\varPsi_{s}^{\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{C}}\right)^{m-1}\,\left(\sup_{a\in\mathbb{A}}\sup_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}}\|\partial^{a}(\varPhi^{\sigma}_{s}-\varPsi^{\sigma}_{s})\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{C}}}\right).

(16.18)

Next, recall that by Corollary
13.11

\|V_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{8,4;1-40\kappa}}\leq C\,,\qquad\|V_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}-V_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{2,3;1-40\kappa}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}.\,

(16.19)

Moreover, by Def.
10.15

\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|V_{s}^{m,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\leq\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|w^{m}_{s}V_{s}^{m,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\\
\leq\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\mathscr{V}^{8,4;1-40\kappa}}\,\lambda_{s}^{1-40\kappa}\,\lambda^{2\kappa m}\,s^{-2+m/2+|a|}\,

(16.20)

and

\sum_{a\in\mathbb{A}^{m}}\sum_{\sigma\in\mathbb{G}^{m}}\|\tilde{w}^{m}_{s}V_{s}^{m,a,\sigma}\|_{\mathscr{M}^{m}}\leq\|V_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{V}}^{2,3;1-40\kappa}}\,\lambda_{s}^{1-40\kappa}\,\lambda^{2\kappa m}\,s^{-2+m/2+|a|}\,.

(16.21)

Note also that
\lambda_{t}\leq\lambda
and

\int_{t}^{1}\lambda_{s}^{1-40\kappa}\,s^{-3/2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C\,\lambda_{t}^{1-40\kappa}\,t^{-1/2},\qquad\int_{t}^{1}\lambda_{s}^{1-41\kappa}\,s^{-3/2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C\,\lambda_{t}^{1-41\kappa}\,t^{-1/2},

(16.22)

for
\lambda\in(0,1]
small enough by Lemma
2.4
(C). The bounds (A), (B), (C), (D), (E) for
\mathbf{Z}^{(2)}_{\varepsilon,\tau;t}
follow now from the estimates gathered above. This finishes the proof.
∎

Corollary 16.8
.

There exists
\lambda_{\star}\in(0,1]
and
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
\lambda\in(0,\lambda_{\star}]
and all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
the map
\mathbf{Z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{U}\to\mathscr{U}
is well-defined and has the unique fixed point denoted by
\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
such that

\|\varPhi_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}-\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{Z}}}\leq C\,\lambda_{\tau\vee\varepsilon}^{\kappa}\,,

(16.23)

where
\varPhi_{{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}:=\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
with
\tau=0
,
\varepsilon=0
. For
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
define

\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon}:=\lim_{s\searrow 0}\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}}.

(16.24)

and
\varPhi=\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon}
with
\tau=0
,
\varepsilon=0
. For all
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
the field
\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon}\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2}_{\tau})^{\mathbb{G}}
is the interacting Gross-Neveu field with the cutoffs
\tau,\varepsilon
, i.e. for all
F\in\mathscr{N}(C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}})
it holds

\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}(F)\equiv\frac{\int F(\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})\,\exp\!\big(-A_{\tau}(\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})+U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})\big)\,\mathrm{d}\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}}{\int\exp\!\big(-A_{\tau}(\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})+U_{\tau,\varepsilon}(\vartheta_{\varepsilon}\ast\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon})\big)\,\mathrm{d}\psi_{\tau,\varepsilon}}=\mathbf{E}F(\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon}).

(16.25)

Moreover,
\varPhi^{\sigma}\in\mathscr{C}^{-1/2}
,
\lim_{\tau,\varepsilon\searrow 0}\|\varPhi^{\sigma}-\varPhi^{\sigma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}}=0
and

\sup_{i\in\{-1,0,1,\ldots\}}(i+2)^{1-40\kappa}\,2^{i/2}\|\Delta_{i}(\varPhi^{\sigma}-\varPsi^{\sigma})\|_{\mathscr{C}}<\infty

(16.26)

for all
\sigma\in\mathbb{G}
and
\alpha\in(-\infty,-1/2)

Remark 16.9
.

Recall that
(\Delta_{i})_{i\in\{-1,0,1,\ldots\}}
are the Littlewood-Paley blocks and the Besov spaces
\mathscr{C}^{\alpha},\tilde{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}
were introduced in Def.
6.19
.

Proof.

The existence and uniqueness of the fixed point
\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
of
\mathbf{Z}_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}\,:\,\mathscr{U}\to\mathscr{U}
for
\lambda\in(0,1]
small enough satisfying the bound
16.23
follows from Theorem
16.7
and the Banach fixed point theorem.

Let
\tau,\varepsilon\in(0,1]
. For
t\in[0,1]
define the functional
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}\in\mathscr{N}(C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}_{\tau}^{2})^{\mathbb{G}})
as in Corollary
14.3
. Note that
U_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}
satisfies the Polchinski equation (
14.33
). Moreover, the fixed point
\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}}
satisfies Eq. (
16.1
). We have
\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;\varepsilon/2}
for
t\in(0,\varepsilon/2]
since
\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}=\varPsi_{\tau,\varepsilon;\varepsilon/2}
for
t\in(0,\varepsilon/2]
by Def.
6.11
and Remark
5.2
(B) and
\dot{G}_{\varepsilon;t}=0
for
t\in(0,\varepsilon]
by Remark
5.2
(A). Consequently, by Prop. 3.10 of
[
DFG22
]
the field
\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon}=\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;\varepsilon/2}
is distributed according to the Gross-Neveu measure
\mu_{\tau,\varepsilon}
with the cutoffs
\tau,\varepsilon
, i.e. the equality (
16.25
) holds true.

The existence of the limit (
16.24
) for
\tau=0
,
\varepsilon=0
follows from the fact that

\Delta_{i}\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;s}=\Delta_{i}\varPhi_{\tau,\varepsilon;t}

(16.27)

for all
\tau,\varepsilon\in[0,1]
,
i\in\{-1,0,1,\ldots\}
and
s,t\in(0,c\,2^{-i})
, where
c\in(0,\infty)
is a universal constant depending only on the choice of the Littlewood-Paley blocks. Since
\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}}\in\mathscr{Z}
using the above observation and Def.
16.1
we conclude that
\varPhi^{\sigma}\in\mathscr{C}^{-1/2}
. Similarly, using the bound (
16.23
) we obtain
\lim_{\tau,\varepsilon\searrow 0}\|\varPhi^{\sigma}-\varPhi^{\sigma}_{\tau,\varepsilon}\|_{\tilde{\mathscr{C}}^{\alpha}}=0
for all
\alpha<-1/2
. Finally, to show the bound (
16.26
) we use the identity
\varPhi_{t}-\varPsi_{t}=\mathbf{Z}_{t}(\varPhi_{\mathchoice{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\displaystyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\textstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}{\mathbin{\vbox{\hbox{\scalebox{.7}{\hskip 0.28453pt$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$\hskip 0.28453pt}}}}}})-\varPsi_{t}
and Theorem
16.7
(E).
∎
